### 2026-10-27T08:50:55Z ZhiQiYang

# 【ChatGPT 審查】PR #353：整卡來源入口與草稿完整性

## 結論

**結論：不建議合併。固定 head 有 6 項應在此切片合入前修正的程式缺陷。** 主要問題是原始草稿會被改寫、來源指紋漏掉整卡身分，以及整卡來源沒有完整接入既有釘選與配置顯示流程。這些問題都能從現有程式和既定契約定位，不需要另訂使用者需求。

本則為審查留言，未修改產品程式、SSOT、任何 🔒 規則或 PR 狀態。

### 固定基準與範圍

- PR：[ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom #353](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353)
- Base：`405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676`
- Head：`986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3`
- Branch：`feat/r01-grid-card-entry-20261027`
- Compare：1 commit，4 個產品檔案，+123 / −15。已讀 PR 正文、完整差異、改動檔案，以及有關的草稿／保存／原生序列化程式。
- 判準：[AGENTS.md](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/AGENTS.md)、公理層 D57／D64／I74／R60、[#249 原完整設計](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/issues/249#issuecomment-6435516955)、[#351 的 2026-10-27 採納紀錄](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/issues/351#issuecomment-6436735068)。
- **已作廢的 R01 動工任務單 `6437090531` 不作驗收依據，也不用它新增的工作阻擋本 PR。** 不以缺少特定新 query API、強制改用 engineering transport、固定 /3 或 /19 號碼本身作為以下 6 項缺陷的理由。

### 證據強度

本輪完成靜態追蹤，另以 Node VM 抽取固定 head 的實際函式，檢查映射初始化、兩次還原、來源指紋、釘選替換、缺少釘選的守門及配置 pitch。探針結果列於文末。這是函式層重現，**不是 CDP、完整 Save/Open 或實機 L4 收據**。PR 作者正文所述 1400×900 操作亦只列為作者自述；其測試環境另用了 #352 的入口，不可自動充當本固定 head 的完整整合驗收。

## 一、阻擋合併的程式缺陷

### R353-01 · P1：重開草稿時，未知／未完成的欄位映射會被自動改成現有欄位

**位置：** [grid-system-workspace.js:543–554](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L543-L554)、[同檔:575–582](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L575-L582)。

還原流程先在 545 行呼叫帶 `prefer` 的 `syncCard`，因此能暫時保留已不存在的欄位，例如 `igCardU="999"`。但 553 行隨即再呼叫一次不帶 `prefer` 的 `syncCard`；578–579 行改選首欄／第二欄，580 行又只有在有 `prefer` 時才補回未知選項。於是保存的 `u=999,v=8` 重開後會變成 `u=3,v=8`。空的未完成映射也會被填滿。

接著 564 行結束初始化並 `capture`，119 行將此變動寫回草稿。使用者只是打開草稿，原始映射就已失去；是否仍被舊 lost pin 擋住提交，不能抵銷這項資料改寫。

**契約：** [D57 的 raw 原樣保留、過期／永久缺失規則](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/公理層.md#L1010-L1027)，及 #249 GU09。既有 `restoreRaw` 本來就專門保留未知值，這次新增的第二次同步破壞了它。

**最小修法：** 將「建立選項／顯示有效值」與「使用者變更映射」分開。所有重畫、ParameterUI 綁定完成後同步及重開，都必須保留 raw 中的未知值與空值，展示待重新選取狀態；只有明確的使用者選取能改映射。不要用有效值、第一欄或第二欄補成新定義。

**回歸條件：** 保存含空映射、未知 fieldId 與失效欄位的草稿，重開後逐字比較三個整卡 raw 欄位；原值不變、不可預覽，明選其他合法來源後才恢復可提交。

### R353-02 · P1：來源指紋沒有包含 cardReference，同值對調／換卡漏判過期

**位置：** [feature-attachments.js:26–30](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/feature-attachments.js#L26-L30)。使用處包括 [grid-system-workspace.js:47–53](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L47-L53)、[134–150](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L134-L150)、[1105–1111](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L1105-L1111)。

本 PR 更新了只供表單比較的 `sourceFormKey`，但真正用來生成來源 pin、重開核對及 `stillSame` 的 `F.gridSourceKey` 仍不含 `cardReference`。原生序列化把卡 id／雙 fieldId 放在獨立的頂層 `cardReference`；受控軸只含 `effectiveStep/effectivePanel` 和 card 來源標記，沒有可替代映射身分的內容。

因此在數值相同時，`{parameterId:10,uFieldId:3,vFieldId:8}` 改為 `{10,8,3}`，甚至改為另一張值相同的卡 `{20,3,8}`，`gridSourceKey` 仍相等。已保存的編輯草稿可在正式來源映射改變後重開，卻不會因來源映射變更而標為 stale；舊 raw 可能覆寫後來的正式映射。開啟中的 nativeChanged 事件守門不能替代跨關閉／重開的持久來源指紋。

**契約：** [D64／I74 的明確來源身分](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/公理層.md#L1107-L1113)、[D57 新鮮度](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/公理層.md#L1017-L1027)，及 #249 GU10。數值相同不等於來源相同。

**最小修法：** 為新的整卡草稿／來源身分納入明確的 parameterId、uFieldId、vFieldId，與既有軸定義及規則一起計算。所有持久 pin、重新取得與讀取守門須使用同一版本化表示；舊 /1、/2 的歷史指紋只按原讀取規則處理，不能無聲重算並假定使用者已核對。

**回歸條件：** 真正相同數值的兩欄對調、同名同值另一張卡替換，都要造成不同來源 key；舊草稿重開為 stale，明確重新取得前不准提交。改卡片畫布位置則不應改定義身分。

### R353-03 · P1：整卡 raw 完整但缺少雙欄 pins 的草稿，仍能通過發布前守門

**位置：** [grid-system-workspace.js:57–63](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L57-L63)、[134–153](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L134-L153)；入口資料驗證為 [feature-attachments.js:219–241](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/feature-attachments.js#L219-L241)。

新增的 `igCard/igCardU/igCardV` 可以與空 `inputRefs` 一起通過 `validateGridDraft`。允許不完整 raw 暫存本身沒有問題；問題是發布前 141 行仍只把名稱以 `Source` 結尾的舊逐欄選擇列為必需 pins，三個整卡欄位全被漏掉。恢復草稿又特意不自動重新釘選。結果，具有完整整卡映射但沒有其歷史來源證據的草稿，可以通過 `validateDraftPins`。

原生最後一關會驗「此刻這張卡是否合法」，但無法補回草稿當時的 typed pins、是否過期與是否曾刪除。不能把「當前同編號內容有效」視作已經明確重新關聯。這是發布前缺少驗證的具體路徑；本輪已重現 schema 與發布守門通過，尚未以瀏覽器做完整發布。

**最小修法：** 發布整卡選擇時，必須要求與 raw 中 parameterId／兩個 fieldId 精確對應的兩份非 lost `parameter-field` pins，並核對其 fingerprint。不完整 raw 可以照常保存；證據缺失時要求使用者明確重新選取，不在 restore 或 save 自動補證據。

**回歸條件：** 用合法有限 raw、完整卡映射但缺一份或兩份 pins 的保存檔，確認能保留原始輸入，而預覽／提交必須 fail closed。補齊明選後才通過。

### R353-04 · P2：初次選卡或換卡會按欄位順序自動決定 u/v

**位置：** [grid-system-workspace.js:33–46](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L33-L46)、[575–579](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L575-L579)。

`fieldOptions` 沒有「請選擇」空項；`syncCard` 在沒有映射時直接用 `ids[0]` 和 `ids[1]`。使用者只選一張卡，UI 就把第一欄當 u、第二欄當 v。換另一張卡若剛好沿用相同 fieldId，也會直接沿用舊卡的欄號，沒有要求重新明選。

這不是一般預設值偏好：[D64 明文禁止依欄位顯示順序推定映射](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/公理層.md#L1107-L1113)，#249 亦明寫即使只有兩欄仍由使用者分別選取。本輪探針中，未指定任何映射就得到 u=3、v=8。

**最小修法：** 新選卡與換卡時，兩個 mapping 控制保留空值，等待各自明選；現有正式物件／保存草稿才依其精確映射還原。兩欄未齊時可保存 raw，但預覽保持不可用。不要以文字提示代替真正的未完成狀態。

**回歸條件：** 以名稱、欄序與尺寸大小相互錯開的卡測試；只選卡後兩軸仍未選，明選 u←1830、v←915 後才取得該映射。換卡後重新選兩軸；單純重畫不重設既有明選。

### R353-05 · P2：恢復草稿後換卡，舊卡 pins 永遠殘留，可能讓合法替換無法完成

**位置：** [grid-system-workspace.js:545–550](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L545-L550)、[609–617](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L609-L617)、[1214–1216](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L1214-L1216)。

`cardPins` 只移除記在 `s.cardPinKeys` 的舊 pins。新建工作區會初始化這個集合；但恢復草稿只載入 `inputPins`，沒有從保存的整卡 raw 還原 `cardPinKeys`，而 547–550 行又略過 restored 分支。

重開選了卡 10 的草稿，再明選卡 20，結果會留下卡 10 的兩份 pins，並加上卡 20 的兩份 pins。即使 raw 已經只使用卡 20，卡 10 後來改值仍使草稿過期；卡 10 曾被刪除則被 lost 守門永久擋住。再換其他卡，只會替換新追蹤的 pins，最早殘留的兩份仍在。

既有 `ReferenceWorkspace.setGridDraft` 對 lost 的保護仍有效；**本項不是聲稱它會讓同 id 重建復活，而是合法改指另一張卡仍無法清除舊來源責任。**

**最小修法：** 從已保存 raw 與已保存 pins 還原兩個受控欄位的擁有關係，不重新取得今日內容。明選另一卡時，以既有 lost／不同物件替換規則原子替換雙欄；其他 phase／gap／anchor 等仍在使用的共享 pins 必須保留。不能藉修此 bug 刪掉 lost 守門。

**回歸條件：** 保存→新 context 重開→改選 B→刪除原本未再使用的 A；草稿不可仍依賴 A。若 A 已 lost，明選不同卡 B 可完成替換；同卡另一欄／同 id 重建仍拒絕。

### R353-06 · P2：整卡來源進入配置畫面，格線與定位仍以缺失的 literal 長度運算，得到 NaN

**位置：** [grid-system-workspace.js:246–251](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L246-L251)、[793–794](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L793-L794)、[883](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L883)、[920–927](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L920-L927)。原生資料形狀見 [grid_system_bridge.inc:16–27](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid_system_bridge.inc#L16-L27)。

來源表單路徑新增了 card 的顯示取值，但配置模式的 `sourceDraft` 直接回傳原生 `s.source`。card 軸序列化只有 `effectiveStep` 或 `effectivePanel`；`def` 卻仍讀 `d.step`／`d.panel`。repeat 得到 undefined pitch，repeat-panel 得到 NaN。畫格線到 926 行會發生 `BigInt(NaN)`；定位也會把 camera 算成 NaN。

所以新建整卡來源後，按畫面既有「選邊界建立配置」，或重開使用整卡的配置，顯示、點格選 cell 與定位不能正常工作。這不代表手填 cell 的所有原生配置提交必定失敗；本項針對已經確定的互動顯示路徑缺陷。

**最小修法：** 統一配置／來源顯示的原生有效軸讀取，分清受控 card 與 literal。有效值只用於顯示與座標投影，不寫回原始直接值、不移除 cardReference。可以沿現有路徑作最小修復，不必為解此錯誤先新增另一套 transport。

**回歸條件：** repeat、repeat-panel 與 mixed mode 的真整卡來源，都走正式「建立配置」及重開配置；格距、選格及定位均為有限值，與原生查詢一致，保存後定義仍是唯一 CardReference。

## 二、須補正事項與交付邊界

以下與上述 6 項分開，不用未採納的新動工任務單擴大產品範圍。

### 1. 減項尚未落實及登錄；原設計已有可執行的合併方向

原 #249 設計的減項是：把兩個受控長度的舊逐欄來源入口，合併到同一「長度來源」區，逐欄模式也在該處選直接值／ParameterRef。現在 [439 行](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L439) 新增整區，但 [587–595 行](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L587-L595) 只在整卡模式把原控制設為 disabled，未完成原設計的合併；逐欄模式仍增加一列。

PR 自述也把 GEO-219 列為未完成。按 [AGENTS.md:279–285](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/AGENTS.md#L279-L285)，合入前需在 BACKLOG 登記實際移除／合併項，並讓畫面與登記一致。**可先照已採納設計完成合併，不必把 R01 整卡需求重新交給使用者決定。** 只有要保留原設計以外的只增不減例外，才需要另走現行規則。

### 2. 選項應可辨認來源身分與型別，現在只顯示名字／數值

[33–46 行](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L33-L46) 只按欄位數至少 2 篩卡，不檢查是否有兩個 dimension，欄位清單也不區分 count／ratio。整數值的 count 會被本地 `cardInt` 當可顯示長度，直到原生預覽才拒絕。核心仍為最後守門，因此這不是已證明非法正式狀態落地。

卡片可同名，卻在選卡與 [確認摘要](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/reference-ui.js#L184-L192) 省略已知卡 id／fieldId；同名、同欄名、同值的兩張卡互換時，前後文字可能完全相同。應補穩定 id 與型別；非 dimension 可顯示原因並禁止完成選取，不能只按數值是否為整數推斷型別。

### 3. 新 raw 寫入舊 schema 的相容界線需要交代

[feature-attachments.js:219–241](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/feature-attachments.js#L219-L241) 在原 /1、/2 白名單加入三個整卡欄位，capability 仍只取 [原 schema](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3/geometry-web/feature-attachments.js#L334-L338)。舊 reader 的有限白名單會拒絕這些欄位；因此新 raw 檔案不能宣稱與舊 /1、/2 完全相容。

本項不要求固定採用 /3、/19 的號碼。應以明確版本／能力或等效、可驗證的相容措施區分新內容，保留舊草稿讀取規則，並寫清 committed card、raw-only card draft、兩者並存與舊 reader 的讀寫矩陣。現有舊 reader 會拒絕，而非已證明靜默丟失資料；不要把這項相容界線誇成已發生資料遺失。

### 4. 尚未交付與尚未驗證須分開記錄

| 項目 | 本輪判斷 |
|---|---|
| 選卡、映射／對調的可見入口 | 程式已有入口；上列缺陷未修，不宜列為已完成的正常操作切片。 |
| 確認／取消路徑 | 本 PR 沿用同一套 `line preview`／`line confirm` 與既有 feature confirmation；未見將 engineering token 錯交 line confirm 的新增程式。不能只因沒改用 engineering 就新增阻擋。 |
| 整組解除 | PR 明載未提供，原生及原設計已有此能力。可以明列後續切片；不能把目前入口宣稱成 #249 全部 UI 已完成，也不用它單獨強迫此次新增整套重構。 |
| Save/Open、取消不清 raw、原子 Undo/Redo | PR 的 7 步自述不等於真完整保存檔／新 context／新服務回讀證據。需補相應產品操作與原生讀回；本輪函式探針不能替代。 |
| 窄畫面、鍵盤、入口移除反證 | 正文列瀏覽器測試及突變尚未做。本稿沒有此類實跑收據，不填為 pass。 |
| CI、架構清單 | 按現有最終 SHA 收據與適用 job 規則處理。PR 自述帳單阻擋與未重產清單，不能據此跳過既定要求，也不增設無關全套重跑。 |

## 三、最小回歸與驗證交接

以下是應對應的行為案例。瀏覽器測試另以【ChatGPT 實作】留言交付本機 Claude 套用；本表不表示已完成 CDP 執行：

| 行為 | 必要斷言 |
|---|---|
| 新選卡／換卡 | 不自動給 u/v；兩欄須各自明選。 |
| 同值對調／換同值另一卡 | source key 改變；保存的舊草稿 stale；取消保留 raw 與正式來源。 |
| 未完成／未知 raw 保存重開 | 三個整卡欄位逐字保留；不可被第一／第二欄替換。 |
| 缺一份／兩份整卡 pins | 原始輸入可保存，預覽／提交拒絕；明確重新選取才補齊。 |
| 恢復草稿後改指另一卡 | 未再使用的舊卡 pins 不殘留；既有 lost 同卡禁止仍有效。 |
| 整卡來源建立／重開配置 | repeat／repeat-panel／mixed 的格距、選格、定位有效；原生來源身分不展平。 |
| 正常確認後保存重開 | 真完整專案、新 context 回讀 CardReference、雙來源種類、規則與剩餘 raw；同一步確認／清稿可 Undo/Redo。 |

修補後針對上述已知風險與現行必跑 gate 驗證即可；不因純稿件整理、證據搬移或相同 head 再追加重複產品驗收。

## 附錄：本輪實際執行的函式探針

使用 Node v24.22.1，從固定 head 閱讀副本抽取實際函式；最小 DOM stub 僅模擬 select 選項行為。產品函式未改寫。讀取副本比 GitHub blob 多一個結尾 LF；移除該 LF 後，三個相關 JS 的 Git blob SHA 與固定 head 完全吻合，函式內容及行號一致。

實際輸出：

```text
source_identity {"swapKeyEqual":true,"cardChangeKeyEqual":true}
native_card_binding {"pitch":null,"error":"The number NaN cannot be converted to a BigInt because it is not an integer"}
new_card_mapping {"u":"3","v":"8"}
restored_unknown_mapping {"afterPrefer":["999","8"],"afterSecondSync":["3","8"]}
restored_change_card_pins ["parameter-field:10:3","parameter-field:10:8","parameter-field:20:3","parameter-field:20:8"]
schema_validation {"accepted":true,"schema":"grid-editor-draft/1","refs":0}
{"fullCardRawWithoutPinsAccepted":true}
```

`pitch:null` 是探針將實際 `undefined` 正規化為可顯示 JSON 的結果。以上證明具體函式分支與守門結果；尚未執行的整合操作，不因本探針轉記成產品驗收通過。

## 【ChatGPT 實作】PR #353 瀏覽器測試 v1 · 第 1/3 則

這是 PR #353 的可套用測試程式 v1，依 [本輪審查](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6437573750)、#249 原採納設計及 #351 的 10-27 交付落點編寫。已作廢的 R01 動工任務單不作新增要求來源。

三則按 1→2→3 依序套用。**新測試檔超過一則容量：第 1 則建立前半，第 2 則以帶上下文的 diff 追加後半；兩則未齊前不要做語法檢查或執行。** 第 3 則交共用突變驗證器、5 個突變與測試架構清單。把三則所有 diff 區塊依序串成單一 patch 也可一次套用；本輪已驗此方式的套用結果與完整檔案相同。

`GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_ONLY=1` 跑新段 P353；full 模式也固定載入。`GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE` 可選精確 ID，但只允許 ONLY=1，不能悄悄讓 full run 少跑。既有案例、既有斷言及 golden 不變。

| 案例 | 主要斷言 |
|---|---|
| entry、explicit_mapping | 真工作區入口、915/1830、受控長度唯讀、390px、兩軸須明選。 |
| commit_cancel、swap、equal_swap | 真候選／確認／取消、唯一 CardReference、對調不改其它軸設定、同值對調仍改來源身分。 |
| reopen、binding_effective | 正式來源重開；整卡有效軸值可畫格、定位、建立配置及重開配置。 |
| native_rejection | 同欄／跨卡／錯型別的核心拒絕；完整 raw 缺雙 pins 仍不得發布。 |
| raw_roundtrip、archive_roundtrip | 真 `.gcanvas` 下載、另開新專案後正式選檔讀回；保留原始字串、未知 fieldId、型別 pins 與正式整卡來源。 |
| history、lost_source、stale_preview | 確認／清稿一步 Undo-Redo；同 id 重建不能重接；明選另一卡釋放舊 pins；過期候選不得覆蓋新世界。 |

Save/Open 使用真產品入口與真封裝檔；測試明確關閉 `showSaveFilePicker` 能力，走產品既有的瀏覽器下載 fallback，再以 CDP file chooser 放入實際檔案。沒有拿 mock 儲存成功取代保存。未知映射／缺 pins 的檔案以既有 `pack` 重新封裝，屬明示反例夾具。

每案輸出 `P353_CASE_RESULT`。純斷言失敗由原 harness 正常產生 `completed:true`／exit 1；前置、未完成請求、例外與 cleanup 錯誤不算 killed。審查的六個缺陷都有對應案例，會保留正確預期而報紅。

本輪只完成語法、固定 blob、精確基底套用與突變單點／語法檢查，尚未執行 App/CDP、原生候選、Save/Open 或突變實跑。

```diff
diff --git a/geometry-web/grid_card_entry_test.js b/geometry-web/grid_card_entry_test.js
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/grid_card_entry_test.js
@@ -0,0 +1,374 @@
+/* PR #353 / R01: trusted browser input, native candidates and formal .gcanvas I/O.
+ * Baseline: 986c5b97125a10f76fa5df8636ae1b2d8ca0ead3.
+ * Fixture writes are explicit and isolated; tested grid edits use visible controls.
+ * No expected failure is converted to pass. Run via cdp_test.js, never standalone.
+ */
+'use strict';
+const fs = require('fs'), os = require('os'), path = require('path');
+const {isDeepStrictEqual: same} = require('util');
+const cdp = require('./cdp_helpers.js');
+const P = require('./project-store.js'), zip = require('./vendor/fflate/umd/index.js');
+const CASE_IDS = Object.freeze([
+  'entry', 'explicit_mapping', 'commit_cancel', 'swap', 'equal_swap', 'reopen',
+  'native_rejection', 'raw_roundtrip', 'archive_roundtrip', 'history',
+  'lost_source', 'stale_preview', 'binding_effective'
+]);
+function selectCases(value) {
+  if (value === undefined) return [...CASE_IDS];
+  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) throw Error('Empty GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE');
+  const ids = value.split(',').map(id => id.trim());
+  if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length || ids.some(id => !CASE_IDS.includes(id)))
+    throw Error('GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE must contain unique exact IDs: ' + CASE_IDS.join(','));
+  return ids;
+}
+function failure(kind, assertion, message, detail) {
+  const error = new Error(message + (detail === undefined ? '' : ' ' + JSON.stringify(detail)));
+  error.p353 = {kind, assertion};
+  return error;
+}
+module.exports = async function gridCardEntryTests({
+  send, eval_, sleep, click, waitForUI, expectNativeDialog, exceptionCount
+}) {
+  if (process.env.GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE !== undefined && process.env.GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_ONLY !== '1')
+    throw failure('precondition','CASE_FILTER_REQUIRES_ONLY','GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE requires GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_ONLY=1');
+  const selected = selectCases(process.env.GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE);
+  console.log('=== P353. R01 whole-card entry, identity, draft and project roundtrip ===');
+  const read = cdp.makeRead(eval_);
+  const page = (fn, ...args) => read('(' + fn.toString() + ')(' + args.map(arg => JSON.stringify(arg)).join(',') + ')');
+  const wait = cdp.makeWait(waitForUI, 20000);
+  const recordCheck = cdp.makeCheck({prefix:'P353 ', throwOnFail:false});
+  const check = (code, value, detail) => {
+    recordCheck(code, value, detail);
+    if (value !== true) throw failure('assertion', code, code, detail);
+  };
+  const need = (code, value, detail) => {
+    if (value !== true) throw failure('precondition', code, code, detail);
+  };
+  const ctl = cdp.makeCtl({eval_, sleep, click}, {
+    scroll:'center', attempts:12, pollMs:60, settleMs:100, missing:'P353 missing ',
+    unreachable:(selector, point) => 'P353 unreachable ' + selector + ' ' + JSON.stringify(point),
+    before:cdp.openCreateObjectMenu(read, selector => ctl(selector))
+  });
+  const key = cdp.makeKey({send, sleep});
+  const fill = cdp.makeFill({ctl, key, send, sleep}, {afterMs:50});
+  const history = cdp.makeHistoryOracle(read, 'P353');
+  let pins = [], nativeTokens = [], currentCase = null;
+  const query = cmd => page(async command => {
+    const result = await apiRequest(command);
+    if (!result.ok) throw Error(command + ': ' + JSON.stringify(result));
+    return result;
+  }, cmd);
+  const dump = async () => (await query('dump')).text;
+  const objects = () => query('objects');
+  const raw = () => page(() => {
+    const all = ReferenceWorkspace.readGridDrafts().drafts;
+    const id = document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace')?.dataset.draftId;
+    return all.find(draft => draft.draftId === id) || (all.length === 1 ? all[0] : null);
+  });
+  const allRaw = () => page(() => ReferenceWorkspace.readGridDrafts());
+  const settled = () => wait("connection.phase==='ready'&&!oc.busy&&!ReferenceWorkspace.busy");
+  const pinHistory = async () => { const pin = await history.capture(); pins.push(pin); return pin; };
+  const releasePin = async pin => { await history.release(pin); pins = pins.filter(item => item !== pin); };
+  const visible = selector => page(s => {
+    const n = document.querySelector(s), r = n?.getBoundingClientRect();
+    return !!n && !!r && r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && getComputedStyle(n).visibility !== 'hidden';
+  }, selector);
+  const choose = async (selector, value) => {
+    const wanted = String(value);
+    const index = await page((s, v) => {
+      const node = document.querySelector(s);
+      if (!node) throw Error('No select: ' + s);
+      return [...node.options].filter(option => !option.disabled).findIndex(option => option.value === v);
+    }, selector, wanted);
+    need('SELECT_OPTION_AVAILABLE', index >= 0, {selector, value:wanted});
+    await ctl(selector);
+    await key('Home', 'Home', 36);
+    for (let n = 0; n < index; n++) await key('ArrowDown', 'ArrowDown', 40);
+    await key('Enter', 'Enter', 13);
+    await wait('document.querySelector(' + JSON.stringify(selector) + ')?.value===' + JSON.stringify(wanted));
+  };
+  const mapping = () => page(() => ({
+    card:document.getElementById('igCard')?.value,
+    u:document.getElementById('igCardU')?.value,
+    v:document.getElementById('igCardV')?.value
+  }));
+  const map = async (id, u, v) => {
+    await choose('#igCard', id);
+    await choose('#igCardU', u);
+    await choose('#igCardV', v);
+    await settled();
+  };
+  const leave = async () => {
+    if (await page(() => !!document.querySelector('.reference-impact[open]'))) await cancel();
+    if (await page(() => !!document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace')?.open)) {
+      await ctl('#igClose');
+      await wait("!document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace')?.open");
+    }
+  };
+  const newProject = async () => {
+    await leave();
+    const before = await page(() => ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectState().id);
+    const dirty = await page(() => ReferenceWorkspace.context().dirty);
+    await ctl('#fileMenuBtn');
+    await expectNativeDialog(() => ctl('#btnNewProject'), {
+      message:'建立一份空白新專案，切換為新的專案識別及「未命名專案」。' +
+        (dirty ? '目前尚未保存的變更不會自動寫入檔案。' : '') +
+        '本次切換可一步復原；僅保存在本頁的形狀特徵參數不會恢復。確定繼續嗎？',
+      accept:true
+    });
+    await wait('ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectState().id!==' + JSON.stringify(before));
+    await settled();
+    const state = await page(() => ({
+      id:ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectState().id,
+      blocked:ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectInfo().editingBlocked,
+      drafts:ReferenceWorkspace.readGridDrafts().drafts.length
+    }));
+    const o = await objects();
+    need('ISOLATED_NEW_PROJECT', state.id !== before && !state.blocked && state.drafts === 0 &&
+      o.gridSystems.length === 0 && o.parameters.length === 0, state);
+  };
+  const parameter = (id, a, b, secondType = 'dimension') => ({
+    op:'put-parameter', value:{id, name:'R01 card ' + id + ' <&>', fields:[
+      {id:8, name:'first-listed', type:secondType, input:String(b)},
+      {id:3, name:'second-listed', type:'dimension', input:String(a)},
+      {id:13, name:'extra count', type:'count', input:'4'}
+    ]}
+  });
+  const grid = (id = 4, parameterId = 10, u = 3, v = 8, mixed = false) => ({
+    op:'put-grid-system', value:{id, name:'R01 existing ' + id, mainAlong:'none',
+      u:mixed ? 'repeat-panel card gap 3 from -5' : 'repeat -5 card',
+      v:'repeat 7 card', cardReference:{parameterId, uFieldId:u, vFieldId:v}}
+  });
+  const applyFixture = async operations => {
+    const result = await page(async ops => {
+      const base = await apiRequest('engineering-read');
+      if (!base.ok) throw Error(JSON.stringify(base));
+      const p = await EngineeringAdapter.preview({expectedRevision:base.revision, operations:ops});
+      if (!p.valid) throw Error('Fixture candidate rejected: ' + JSON.stringify(p));
+      const result = await EngineeringAdapter.confirm({token:p.token});
+      if (!result.ok || !await renderObjects()) throw Error('Fixture commit/render failed');
+      return result;
+    }, operations);
+    need('NATIVE_FIXTURE_COMMITTED', result.ok === true, result);
+  };
+  const fixture = async ({source = false, equal = false, mixed = false, board = false} = {}) => {
+    await newProject();
+    const ops = [
+      parameter(10, board ? 915 : 12, board ? 1830 : 30),
+      parameter(20, 12, 12), parameter(30, 12, 30, 'count')

+    if (source) ops.push(grid(4, equal ? 20 : 10, 3, 8, mixed));

+    const o = await objects();
+    need('NATIVE_FIXTURE_CARDS', o.parameters.length === 3, o.parameters.map(row => row.id));
+    if (source) {
+      const g = o.gridSystems.find(row => row.id === 4);
+      need('NATIVE_FIXTURE_CARD_SOURCE', g?.cardReference?.parameterId === (equal ? 20 : 10) &&

+    }
+    await page(() => {
+      if (window.__p353ApiTrace) throw Error('Trace leaked from another case');
+      const trace = window.__p353ApiTrace = {original:window.apiRequest, calls:[]};
+      window.apiRequest = async function(command, ...args) {
+        const row = {command, state:'pending'};
+        trace.calls.push(row);
+        try {
+          const result = await trace.original.call(this, command, ...args);
+          row.result = structuredClone(result); row.state = 'fulfilled';

+    });
+  };

+    await wait("document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace')?.open&&document.getElementById('igName')&&!document.getElementById('igName').disabled");
+    check('ENTRY_CARD_VISIBLE', await visible('#igCardRef') && await visible('#igCard'));

+  const healthy = async code => {

+      response:await apiRequest('objects')
+    }));
+    if (state.connection !== 'ready' || state.ready !== 'complete' || state.response?.ok !== true)
+      throw failure('exception', code + '_ENVIRONMENT', 'Application/native service is not healthy', state);
+  };
+  const waitProduct = async (code, expression) => {
+    try { await wait(expression); }

+      // Only a completed, responsive application can produce a product timeout.
+      // CDP/protocol errors and unfinished native requests are never mutation kills.

+      const pending = await page(() => window.__p353ApiTrace?.calls.filter(row => row.state === 'pending') || []);
+      if (pending.length) throw failure('exception',code + '_PENDING_NATIVE','Native request did not complete',pending);
+      await healthy(code);
+      check(code, false, {timeout:true,expression});
+    }
+  };
+  const preview = async (prefix, {button = '#igSaveSource', command = 'line preview grid-system '} = {}) => {
+    check(prefix + '_PREVIEW_ENABLED', await page(selector => {
+      const b = document.querySelector(selector); return !!b && !b.disabled;
+    },button));
+    const cursor = await page(() => window.__p353ApiTrace.calls.length);
+    await ctl(button);
+    const completed = '(window.__p353ApiTrace.calls.slice(' + cursor + ').some(row=>' +
+      'row.command.startsWith(' + JSON.stringify(command) + ')&&row.state!=="pending"))';
+    await waitProduct(prefix + '_NATIVE_PREVIEW', completed);

+      .find(row => row.command.startsWith(cmd) && row.state !== 'pending'), cursor, command);
+    if (result?.state === 'rejected') throw failure('exception',prefix + '_PREVIEW_REQUEST_REJECTED',result.error);
+    await healthy(prefix + '_PREVIEW');

+    check(prefix + '_PREVIEW_VALID', result.result.preview.valid === true, result.result.preview);

+    check(prefix + '_IMPACT_VISIBLE', await visible('.reference-impact[open]'));
+    return result.result.preview;
+  };
+  const confirm = async () => {

+      await page(() => document.getElementById('impact-confirm').disabled === false));

+    await wait("!document.querySelector('.reference-impact[open]')");
+    await settled();
+    await wait("!document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace')?.open||!document.getElementById('igName')?.disabled");
+  };
+  const rejectedAttempt = async code => {
+    if (await page(() => !document.getElementById('igSaveSource').disabled)) await ctl('#igSaveSource');
+    // A mutation that wrongly reaches a confirmation is an assertion failure,
+    // not an artificial wait for the still-open modal to become idle.
+    await waitProduct(code + '_FINISHED', "!!document.querySelector('.reference-impact[open]')||(!document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace').classList.contains('ig-busy')&&!oc.busy&&!ReferenceWorkspace.busy)");

+  };
+  async function cancel() {

+    ['u','v'].every(axis => {

+    try {

+        if (files.length > 1) throw failure('exception', 'AMBIGUOUS_DOWNLOAD', 'Multiple project downloads', files);
+        if (files.length === 1) { file = path.join(directory, files[0]); break; }
+        await sleep(100);
+      }
+    } finally {
+      await page(() => {
+        const saved = window.__p353SavePicker;
+        if (saved?.descriptor) Object.defineProperty(window, 'showSaveFilePicker', saved.descriptor);
+        else delete window.showSaveFilePicker;
+        delete window.__p353SavePicker;
+      });
+    }
+    check('FORMAL_SAVE_FILE', !!file && fs.statSync(file).size > 0, {directory, file});

+    return {file, directory, bytes, loaded};
+  };
+  const openFile = async (file, expectedProjectId) => {
+    await newProject();
```

```diff
+      check('RAW_TYPED_PINS', [3,8].every(id => draft.inputRefs.some(ref => ref.kind === 'parameter-field' &&
+        ref.parameterId === 10 && ref.fieldId === id && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(ref.fingerprint))), draft.inputRefs);

+            ((a === 0 ? geometry.x : geometry.y) + (coordinate-geometry.camera[a])*geometry.scale)) < 0.02)),geometry);
+      await fill('#igCellI','-1'); await fill('#igCellJ','1'); await ctl('#igLocateCell');
+      await ctl('[data-ig-anchor="0,0"]');

+        return !!selected && ['x','y','width','height'].every(key => Number.isFinite(Number(selected.getAttribute(key)))) &&

+      check('BINDING_CANDIDATE_EXACT',!!candidate && candidate.system === 4 && candidate.boundary === 301 &&
+        same(candidate.cell,['-1','1']) && same(candidate.translation,[107,163]) && (await dump()) === before,candidate);
+      await confirm();
+      const saved = (await objects()).gridBindings.filter(row=>row.system === 4 && row.boundary === 301);
+      check('BINDING_COMMIT_EXACT',saved.length === 1 && same(saved[0].cell,['-1','1']) &&
+        same(saved[0].translation,[107,163]) && identity(await source(4),10,3,8),saved);
+      await leave(); await ctl('#btnZoom');
+      await ctl('.ocard[data-kind="grid-binding"][data-id="'+saved[0].id+'"]');
+      await ctl('#gridObjectOpen');
+      await wait("document.getElementById('infiniteGridWorkspace')?.open&&document.getElementById('igVertex')?.value!==''");
+      await waitProduct('BINDING_REOPEN_RESULT', 'document.querySelectorAll("#igResultView [data-ig-result-axis]").length>0');
+      check('BINDING_REOPEN_RESULT', await page(() =>
+        document.getElementById('igCellI').value === '-1' && document.getElementById('igCellJ').value === '1' &&
+        document.querySelectorAll('#igGridView [data-ig-axis="u"]').length > 0 &&
+        document.querySelectorAll('#igGridView [data-ig-axis="v"]').length > 0));
+    }
+  };
+  const cleanup = async () => {
+    for (const token of nativeTokens) await query('engineering-cancel ' + token);
+    nativeTokens = [];
+    if (await page(() => ReferenceWorkspace.featureConfirmationPending())) throw Error('Unsettled feature confirmation');
+    await leave();
+    for (const pin of pins) await history.release(pin);
+    pins = [];
+    await page(() => {
+      const trace = window.__p353ApiTrace;
+      if (trace) {window.apiRequest = trace.original; delete window.__p353ApiTrace;}
+      const picker = window.__p353SavePicker;
+      if (picker) {
+        if (picker.descriptor) Object.defineProperty(window,'showSaveFilePicker',picker.descriptor);
+        else delete window.showSaveFilePicker;
+        delete window.__p353SavePicker;
+      }
+      delete window.__p353FileChooser;
+    });
+    await send('Page.setInterceptFileChooserDialog',{enabled:false});
+    await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride',{width:1400,height:900,deviceScaleFactor:1,mobile:false});
+    await settled();
+  };
+  const results = [];
+  for (const id of selected) {
+    currentCase = id;
+    const baselineExceptions = exceptionCount();
+    let result = {id,status:'pass',kind:null,assertion:null,error:null};
+    try {
+      await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride',{width:1400,height:1000,deviceScaleFactor:1,mobile:false});
+      await cases[id]();
+      if (exceptionCount() !== baselineExceptions)
+        throw failure('exception','UNCAUGHT_BROWSER_EXCEPTION','Browser exception count changed',
+          {before:baselineExceptions,after:exceptionCount()});
+    } catch (error) {
+      const kind = error.p353?.kind || 'exception';
+      result = {id,status:kind === 'assertion' ? 'fail' : 'error',kind,
+        assertion:error.p353?.assertion || 'UNEXPECTED_EXCEPTION',error:error.message};
+    }
+    if (exceptionCount() !== baselineExceptions) {
+      result = {id,status:'error',kind:'exception',assertion:'UNCAUGHT_BROWSER_EXCEPTION',
+        error:'Browser exception count changed: ' + baselineExceptions + ' -> ' + exceptionCount(),
+        ...(result.status !== 'pass' ? {firstFailure:result} : {})};
+    }
+    const cleanupExceptions = exceptionCount();
+    try {
+      await cleanup();
+      if (exceptionCount() !== cleanupExceptions) throw Error('Browser exception during cleanup');
+    }
+    catch (error) {
+      result = {id,status:'error',kind:'cleanup',assertion:'CLEANUP_FAILED',error:error.message,
+        ...(result.status !== 'pass' ? {firstFailure:result} : {})};
+    }
+    results.push(result);
+    console.log('P353_CASE_RESULT ' + JSON.stringify(result));
+    if (result.kind === 'cleanup') break; // The next case cannot claim an isolated precondition.
+  }
+  // Assertion failures have already entered the harness failN. Let its footer
+  // finish with completed:true and exit 1 so a healthy mutation can be killed.
+  // Environment/precondition/exception/cleanup failures remain incomplete runs.
+  if (results.some(result => result.status === 'error') || results.length !== selected.length)
+    throw Error('P353 cases failed: ' + results.filter(result => result.status !== 'pass').map(result =>
+      result.id + '/' + result.kind + '/' + result.assertion).join(','));
+};
+module.exports.caseIds = CASE_IDS;
+module.exports.selectCases = selectCases;
```

## 【ChatGPT 實作】PR #353 瀏覽器測試 v1 · 第 3/3 則

本版交付 5 個精確單點突變，各在固定 head 恰好命中一次：

| 突變 | 必須報紅的案例／斷言 |
|---|---|
| 隱藏整卡來源入口 | entry／ENTRY_CARD_VISIBLE。 |
| 對調按鈕變 no-op | swap／SWAP_MAPPING_ONLY。 |
| 把受控 card token 展平成有效數字 | commit_cancel／CREATE_NATIVE_PREVIEW 或 CREATE_PREVIEW_VALID，須有真完成回覆與健康檢查。 |
| 不保存三個整卡 raw 欄位 | raw_roundtrip／RAW_SOURCE_MAPPING。 |
| 不保存雙欄 typed pins | raw_roundtrip／RAW_TYPED_PINS。 |

審查中已存在的六個缺陷仍由正常案例抓出。修正後若程式位置改動，精確 needle 不符會回 blocked，須依新 head 更新突變位置，不做模糊替換。

共用 `browser_mutation_check.py` 以暫存快照執行，為每次 baseline／mutant／restored 啟動自己的服務、port、Chrome profile 與 feedback 目錄，先核對 runtime fingerprint。只支援原生 Linux／WSL 的 Linux Python、Node、Chrome 與原生執行檔；Windows Python 的程序樹清理未實作，會在啟動前拒絕。

每次結束清理本次程序群並核對沒有活程序。只有「完整 baseline 綠 → 指定具名斷言紅 → 還原綠」才記 killed。未啟動、timeout、語法錯、前置失敗、cleanup 失敗、單案的意外 transport 錯誤，都記 blocked。收據含來源檔雜湊、原生二進位雜湊、實際指令、退出碼、原始日誌、突變前後雜湊與清理結果。

本次架構清單只登記新增測試檔的 exclusion 與更新 cdp_test hash。產品程式的既有架構缺項仍按審查修正，不藉此測試補丁改分類。公理層、正典閱讀器、🔒 規則及既有斷言皆未修改。

```diff
diff --git a/geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py b/geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py
@@ -0,0 +1,492 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Run real CDP controls/mutants in temporary source snapshots (never edit repo).
+
+Requires native Linux (including a Linux Python inside WSL), with /proc.
+Windows process-tree cleanup is not supported and is rejected before launch.
+Each manifest names one existing CDP scope, its complete case IDs, and exact
+single-site source replacements. Native executables must already be built from
+the reported product revision. An assertion failure is a kill only after green
+controls and a completed harness result; startup/fixture/cleanup failures block.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import shlex
+import signal
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import time
+
+
+def digest(data):
+    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+
+
+def safe_path(root, relative):
+    if not isinstance(relative, str) or not relative or Path(relative).is_absolute():
+        raise ValueError('Expected relative repository path')
+    unresolved = root / relative
+    if unresolved.is_symlink():
+        raise ValueError('Snapshot does not follow symlinks: ' + relative)
+    path = unresolved.resolve()
+    if not path.is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
+        raise ValueError('Path escapes snapshot: ' + relative)
+    return path
+
+
+def command(argv, cwd, timeout=30, env=None):
+    return subprocess.run(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace',
+                          stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                          timeout=timeout, check=False)
+
+
+def proc_identity(pid):
+    """Read Linux process identity without treating a reused PID as our child."""
+    try:
+        raw = (Path('/proc') / str(pid) / 'stat').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
+        return None
+    fields = raw[raw.rfind(')') + 2:].split()
+    try:
+        actual_pid = int(raw.split(' ', 1)[0])
+        if actual_pid != int(pid):
+            raise ValueError('PID mismatch')
+        return {'pid': actual_pid, 'state': fields[0], 'group': int(fields[2]),
+                'session': int(fields[3]), 'start': int(fields[19])}
+    except (IndexError, ValueError) as error:
+        raise RuntimeError('Cannot verify Linux process identity: ' + str(pid)) from error
+
+
+def require_process_namespace():
+    if not sys.platform.startswith('linux') or not Path('/proc/self/stat').is_file():
+        raise RuntimeError('Requires native Linux/WSL Python with /proc; Windows owned-tree cleanup is unsupported')
+    # Some hosted executors expose a host /proc alongside virtual child PIDs.
+    # Never use that unrelated process table to decide what may be signalled.
+    self_stat = Path('/proc/self/stat').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    if int(self_stat.split(' ', 1)[0]) != os.getpid():
+        raise RuntimeError('/proc does not describe this process namespace; refusing to launch')
+    current = proc_identity(os.getpid())
+    if current is None or current['group'] != os.getpgrp() or current['session'] != os.getsid(0):
+        raise RuntimeError('/proc process-group/session identity disagrees with the OS; refusing to launch')
+
+
+def remember_owner(process):
+    # Popen(start_new_session=True) makes this child both group and session leader.
+    process.mutation_owner = proc_identity(process.pid)
+    owner = process.mutation_owner
+    if owner is not None and (owner['group'] != process.pid or owner['session'] != process.pid):
+        raise RuntimeError('Child did not acquire its isolated process group')
+
+
+def live_group_members(group):
+    members = []
+    for entry in Path('/proc').iterdir():
+        if not entry.name.isdigit():
+            continue
+        row = proc_identity(int(entry.name))
+        # A zombie holds no executable, socket or profile file; the parent reaps it.
+        if row is not None and row['group'] == group and row['state'] not in ['Z', 'X', 'x']:
+            members.append(row)
+    return members
+
+
+def stop(process):
+    if process is None:
+        return None
+    group = process.pid
+    owner = getattr(process, 'mutation_owner', None)
+    leader = proc_identity(group)
+    if owner is not None and leader is not None and leader['start'] != owner['start']:
+        raise RuntimeError('Owned process-group id was reused; refusing to signal it')
+    sent = []
+    for sig, grace in [(signal.SIGTERM, 5), (signal.SIGKILL, 5)]:
+        # Do not return just because the leader exited: its browser/bridge can live on.
+        if not live_group_members(group):
+            break
+        try:
+            os.killpg(group, sig)
+            sent.append(sig.name)
+        except ProcessLookupError:
+            pass
+        deadline = time.monotonic() + grace
+        while live_group_members(group) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
+            time.sleep(0.05)
+    remaining = live_group_members(group)
+    if remaining:
+        raise RuntimeError('Owned process group still has live members: ' + str(remaining))
+    process.wait(timeout=2)
+    return {'group': group, 'signals': sent, 'liveMembers': 0, 'completed': True}
+
+
+def snapshot(repo, destination, extras):
+    result = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(repo), 'ls-files', '-z'],
+                            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+                            check=True)
+    names = set(os.fsdecode(x) for x in result.stdout.split(b'\0') if x)
+    names.update(extras)
+    files = {}
+    for name in sorted(names):
+        source = safe_path(repo, name)
+        if source.is_symlink():
+            raise ValueError('Snapshot does not follow symlinks: ' + name)
+        if not source.is_file():
+            if name in extras:
+                raise ValueError('Missing test input: ' + name)
+            continue  # A tracked deletion remains a deletion in the snapshot.
+        target = safe_path(destination, name)
+        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        shutil.copy2(source, target)
+        # Attest to the bytes actually executed, not a later read of a live worktree.
+        files[name] = digest(target.read_bytes())
+    return files
+
+
+RESERVED_GEOMETRY_ENV = frozenset([
+    'GEOMETRY_TEST_URL', 'GEOMETRY_FEEDBACK_DIR', 'GEOMETRY_CHROME',
+    'GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS', 'GEOMETRY_CHROME_PROFILE', 'GEOMETRY_CDP_PORT',
+    'GEOMETRY_TEST_SERVER_PATH_STYLE', 'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE', 'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE_EXECUTABLE',
+    'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE_WSL', 'GEOMETRY_MODEL_BASE', 'GEOMETRY_CDP_UPDATE_GOLDEN',
+    'GEOMETRY_CDP_FROM_P31', 'GEOMETRY_TEST_SCREENSHOT',
+    'GEOMETRY_GRID_SCREENSHOT_DIR', 'GEOMETRY_REPAIR_SCREENSHOT_DIR',
+])
+
+
+def subcase_environment(manifest, value):
+    allowed = manifest.get('subcaseVariables', [])
+    reserved = RESERVED_GEOMETRY_ENV | {manifest['scope'], manifest['caseFilter']}
+    if (not isinstance(value, dict) or not set(value).issubset(allowed) or set(value) & reserved or
+            any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item or '\0' in item for item in value.values())):
+        raise ValueError('Mutation env must use declared non-reserved subcase keys and string values')
+    return dict(value)
+
+
+def read_manifest(path):
+    value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+    required = ['schema', 'suite', 'scope', 'caseFilter', 'resultPrefix',
+                'cases', 'inputs', 'mutations']
+    if not isinstance(value, dict) or any(k not in value for k in required):
+        raise ValueError('Incomplete mutation manifest')
+    if value['schema'] != 'geometry-browser-mutations/1':
+        raise ValueError('Unknown mutation manifest schema')
+    for key in ['scope', 'caseFilter']:
+        name = value[key]
+        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.startswith('GEOMETRY_') or not name.replace('_', '').isalnum():
+            raise ValueError('Invalid environment key: ' + key)
+    if value['scope'] == value['caseFilter']:
+        raise ValueError('Scope and case filter must be distinct')
+    variables = value.get('subcaseVariables', [])
+    if (not isinstance(variables, list) or
+            any(not isinstance(name, str) or not name.startswith('GEOMETRY_') or
+                not name.endswith('_ONLY') or not name.isascii() or not name.replace('_', '').isalnum()
+                for name in variables) or len(set(variables)) != len(variables) or
+            set(variables) & (RESERVED_GEOMETRY_ENV | {value['scope'], value['caseFilter']})):
+        raise ValueError('Invalid or reserved subcase variable declaration')
+    for key in ['cases', 'inputs']:
+        items = value[key]
+        if not isinstance(items, list) or not items or any(not isinstance(x, str) or not x for x in items) or len(set(items)) != len(items):
+            raise ValueError('Invalid or duplicate ' + key)
+    ids = set()
+    for mutation in value['mutations']:
+        if not isinstance(mutation, dict) or not {'id', 'case', 'assertions', 'changes'} <= mutation.keys():
+            raise ValueError('Incomplete mutation')
+        mid = mutation['id']
+        if not isinstance(mid, str) or not mid.replace('-', '').replace('_', '').isalnum() or mid in ids:
+            raise ValueError('Invalid/duplicate mutation id')
+        ids.add(mid)
+        if mutation['case'] not in value['cases'] or not mutation['assertions'] or not mutation['changes']:
+            raise ValueError('Mutation lacks a case, assertion or change')
+        subcase_environment(value, mutation.get('env', {}))
+        for change in mutation['changes']:
+            if not {'file', 'before', 'after'} <= change.keys() or not change['before'] or change['before'] == change['after']:
+                raise ValueError('Mutation must replace one nonempty exact site')
+    if not ids:
+        raise ValueError('No mutations requested')
+    return value
+
+
+def parse_result(log, manifest, expected_cases, exit_code, reject_transport=False):
+    cases, footer, malformed = [], [], []
+    for line in log.splitlines():
+        prefix = next((p for p in [manifest['resultPrefix'], 'CDP_RESULT '] if line.startswith(p)), None)
+        if prefix is None:
+            continue
+        try:
+            row = json.loads(line[len(prefix):])
+            if not isinstance(row, dict):
+                raise ValueError('Result is not an object')
+            (footer if prefix == 'CDP_RESULT ' else cases).append(row)
+        except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
+            malformed.append(str(error))
+    errors = []
+    assertions = []
+    ids = [x.get('case', x.get('id')) for x in cases]
+    valid_ids = all(isinstance(value, str) and value for value in ids)
+    if not valid_ids:
+        errors.append('Malformed case id')
+    final = footer[0] if len(footer) == 1 else {}
+    counters = ['passedAssertions', 'failedAssertions', 'uncaughtBrowserExceptions']
+    valid_counters = all(type(final.get(key)) is int and final[key] >= 0 for key in counters)
+    if len(footer) == 1 and (final.get('schema') != 'geometry-cdp-result/1' or not valid_counters):
+        errors.append('Malformed CDP footer schema or counters')
+    complete = (not malformed and valid_ids and len(ids) == len(expected_cases) and
+                set(ids) == set(expected_cases) and len(set(ids)) == len(ids) and
+                len(footer) == 1 and final.get('completed') is True and valid_counters and
+                final.get('uncaughtBrowserExceptions') == 0)
+    for case in cases:
+        status = case.get('status')
+        failures = case.get('failures', [])
+        if not isinstance(failures, list):
+            errors.append('Malformed failures list')
+            continue
+        if status not in ['passed', 'pass', 'failed', 'fail', 'error']:
+            errors.append('Unknown case status')
+        if status == 'error' or case.get('kind') in ['precondition', 'exception', 'cleanup']:
+            errors.append(case)
+        if status in ['passed', 'pass'] and (failures or case.get('kind') == 'assertion'):
+            errors.append('Passing case also reports an assertion failure')
+        if case.get('kind') == 'assertion' and status in ['failed', 'fail']:
+            name = case.get('assertion')
+            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
+                errors.append('Assertion failure has no valid name')
+            else:
+                assertions.append(name)
+        for failure in failures:
+            if (not isinstance(failure, dict) or failure.get('kind') != 'assertion' or
+                    not isinstance(failure.get('name'), str) or not failure['name']):
+                errors.append(failure)
+            else:
+                assertions.append(failure['name'])
+        if status in ['failed', 'fail'] and not failures and case.get('kind') != 'assertion':
+            errors.append('Failure without a named assertion')
+    if assertions and final.get('failedAssertions') == 0:
+        errors.append('Named failures contradict the zero-failure footer')
+    traces = {key: final.get(key) for key in ['firstTransportFailure', 'firstNetworkFailure']}
+    if reject_transport and len(footer) == 1:
+        if any(key not in final for key in traces):
+            errors.append('Single-case result is missing transport trace fields')
+        if any(value is not None for value in traces.values()):
+            errors.append('Unexpected transport/network failure in a mutation control or mutant')
+    green = (complete and not errors and not assertions and exit_code == 0 and
+             all(x.get('status') in ['passed', 'pass'] for x in cases) and
+             final.get('failedAssertions') == 0 and final.get('passedAssertions', 0) > 0)
+    return {'complete': complete, 'green': green, 'cases': cases,
+            'footer': footer, 'errors': errors + malformed,
+            'assertions': assertions, 'exitCode': exit_code, 'transportTraces': traces,
+            'transportPolicy': 'require-null' if reject_transport else 'suite-defined'}
+
+
+SAFE_CHROME_FLAGS = frozenset(['--no-sandbox', '--disable-setuid-sandbox',
+                             '--disable-dev-shm-usage', '--disable-gpu',
+                             '--disable-software-rasterizer'])
+
+
+def isolated_environment(environ):
+    # Only the executable and an explicit safe set of Linux flags may be inherited.
+    # In particular, no caller profile, port, output path, WSL bridge mode, model
+    # executable, fixture filter or golden update can override this run's values.
+    env = {name: value for name, value in environ.items() if not name.startswith('GEOMETRY_')}
+    if environ.get('GEOMETRY_CHROME'):
+        env['GEOMETRY_CHROME'] = environ['GEOMETRY_CHROME']
+    flags = shlex.split(environ.get('GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS', ''))
+    if any(flag not in SAFE_CHROME_FLAGS for flag in flags):
+        raise ValueError('GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS contains a flag outside the isolated-run allowlist')
+    if flags:
+        env['GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS'] = ' '.join(flags)
+    return env
+
+
+def run_cdp(root, manifest, options, output, label, case=None, extra_env=None):
+    run_dir = output / label
+    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
+    env = isolated_environment(os.environ)
+    with socket.socket() as sock:
+        sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
+        port = sock.getsockname()[1]
+        with socket.socket() as browser_sock:
+            browser_sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
+            browser_port = browser_sock.getsockname()[1]
+    env.update({manifest['scope']: '1',
+                'GEOMETRY_TEST_URL': 'http://127.0.0.1:' + str(port) + '/',
+                'GEOMETRY_FEEDBACK_DIR': str(root.parent / ('feedback-' + label)),
+                'GEOMETRY_CHROME_PROFILE': str(root.parent / ('chrome-' + label)),
+                'GEOMETRY_CDP_PORT': str(browser_port),
+                'GEOMETRY_TEST_SERVER_PATH_STYLE': 'native',
+                'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE_EXECUTABLE': str(options.bridge.resolve()),
+                'GEOMETRY_TEST_SCREENSHOT': str(run_dir / 'screens' / 'phase1-shell.png'),
+                'GEOMETRY_GRID_SCREENSHOT_DIR': str(run_dir / 'screens'),
+                'GEOMETRY_REPAIR_SCREENSHOT_DIR': str(run_dir / 'screens')})
+    if case is not None:
+        env[manifest['caseFilter']] = case
+    subcase_env = subcase_environment(manifest, {} if extra_env is None else extra_env)
+    if subcase_env and case is None:
+        raise ValueError('Subcase filters may not narrow the full-suite baseline')
+    env.update(subcase_env)
+    if options.model_base:
+        env['GEOMETRY_MODEL_BASE'] = str(options.model_base.resolve())
+    server = test = None
+    started = time.monotonic()
+    expected = [case] if case else manifest['cases']
+    result = {'complete': False, 'green': False, 'errors': [], 'assertions': []}
+    try:
+        with (run_dir / 'server.log').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as server_log:
+            server = subprocess.Popen([options.python, 'geometry-web/serve.py', str(port)],
+                                      cwd=root, env=env, stdout=server_log,
+                                      stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
+            remember_owner(server)
+            deadline = time.monotonic() + 60
+            probe = None
+            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
+                if server.poll() is not None:
+                    raise RuntimeError('Isolated server exited: ' + str(server.returncode))
+                probe = command([options.python, 'geometry-web/runtime_version.py', '--probe', str(port)],
+                                root, timeout=10, env=env)
+                if probe.returncode == 0:
+                    break
+                time.sleep(0.25)
+            if probe is None or probe.returncode != 0:
+                raise RuntimeError('Runtime fingerprint/ready probe failed: ' + (probe.stdout if probe else 'no probe'))
+            (run_dir / 'runtime.log').write_text(probe.stdout, encoding='utf-8')
+            with (run_dir / 'cdp.log').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as test_log:
+                test = subprocess.Popen([options.node, 'geometry-web/cdp_test.js'],
+                                        cwd=root, env=env, stdout=test_log,
+                                        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
+                remember_owner(test)
+                exit_code = test.wait(timeout=options.timeout)
+            log = (run_dir / 'cdp.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+            # Full-suite controls include intentionally offline cases. All current
+            # mutation cases are non-offline, so both their mutant and restored
+            # controls must have null transport traces. A future offline mutation
+            # needs an explicit expected/unexpected transport protocol first.
+            result = parse_result(log, manifest, expected, exit_code, reject_transport=case is not None)
+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+        result['errors'].append(type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error))
+    finally:
+        result['cleanup'] = []
+        for process in [test, server]:
+            try:
+                cleanup = stop(process)
+                if cleanup is not None:
+                    result['cleanup'].append(cleanup)
+            except (OSError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+                result['errors'].append('cleanup: ' + str(error))
+                result['green'] = False
+        result['elapsedSeconds'] = round(time.monotonic() - started, 3)
+        result['command'] = [options.node, 'geometry-web/cdp_test.js']
+        result['scope'] = manifest['scope']
+        result['caseFilter'] = case
+        result['subcaseEnv'] = subcase_env
+        result['logs'] = str(run_dir)
+    return result
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    parser.add_argument('--repo', type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
+    parser.add_argument('--manifest', type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument('--model-base', type=Path)
+    parser.add_argument('--output-dir', type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument('--python', default=sys.executable)
+    parser.add_argument('--node', default='node')
+    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=900)
+    options = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        require_process_namespace()
+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as error:
+        parser.error(str(error))
+    repo, output = options.repo.resolve(), options.output_dir.resolve()
+    if output.exists():
+        parser.error('--output-dir must be new; old receipts are never overwritten')
+    if output.is_relative_to(repo):
+        parser.error('--output-dir must be outside the product repository')
+    if not options.bridge.is_file() or (options.model_base and not options.model_base.is_file()):
+        parser.error('Pass existing native executable paths')
+    manifest_path = options.manifest.resolve()
+    manifest = read_manifest(manifest_path)
+    output.mkdir(parents=True)
+    report = {'schema': 'geometry-browser-mutation-receipt/1', 'suite': manifest['suite'],
+              'manifestHash': digest(manifest_path.read_bytes()), 'mutations': [],
+              'head': command(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], repo).stdout.strip(),
+              'bridgeHash': digest(options.bridge.read_bytes()), 'status': 'blocked'}
+    if options.model_base:
+        report['modelBaseHash'] = digest(options.model_base.read_bytes())
+    exit_code = 2
+    try:
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='geometry-browser-mutations-') as directory:
+            temporary = Path(directory)
+            baseline = temporary / 'baseline'
+            baseline.mkdir()
+            report['files'] = snapshot(repo, baseline, manifest['inputs'])
+            report['sourceHash'] = digest(json.dumps(report['files'], sort_keys=True).encode())
+            report['baseline'] = run_cdp(baseline, manifest, options, output, 'baseline')
+            if not report['baseline']['green']:
+                report['reason'] = 'Baseline is not green; no mutation counts as killed.'
+                return 2
+            for mutation in manifest['mutations']:
+                subcase_env = subcase_environment(manifest, mutation.get('env', {}))
+                row = {'id': mutation['id'], 'case': mutation['case'], 'status': 'blocked',
+                       'subcaseEnv': subcase_env}
+                report['mutations'].append(row)
+                work = temporary / mutation['id']
+                shutil.copytree(baseline, work)
+                originals = {}
+                try:
+                    for change in mutation['changes']:
+                        target = safe_path(work, change['file'])
+                        data = target.read_bytes()
+                        originals.setdefault(target, data)
+                        before, after = change['before'].encode(), change['after'].encode()
+                        if data.count(before) != 1:
+                            raise ValueError('Exact mutation site drifted: ' + change['file'])
+                        target.write_bytes(data.replace(before, after, 1))
+                        row.setdefault('changes', []).append({**change, 'beforeHash': digest(data),
+                                                            'afterHash': digest(target.read_bytes())})
+                    for target in originals:
+                        if target.suffix == '.js':
+                            syntax = command([options.node, '--check', str(target)], work)
+                            if syntax.returncode != 0:
+                                raise ValueError('Mutant syntax failure: ' + syntax.stdout)
+                    row['mutant'] = run_cdp(work, manifest, options, output, mutation['id'] + '-mutant', mutation['case'], extra_env=subcase_env)
+                except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+                    row['reason'] = str(error)
+                finally:
+                    for target, data in originals.items():
+                        target.write_bytes(data)
+                row['restored'] = run_cdp(work, manifest, options, output, mutation['id'] + '-restored', mutation['case'], extra_env=subcase_env)
+                mutant = row.get('mutant')
+                if not row['restored']['green']:
+                    row['reason'] = 'Restored control is not green.'
+                elif mutant and mutant['green']:
+                    row['status'] = 'survived'
+                elif mutant and mutant['complete'] and not mutant['errors'] and mutant.get('exitCode') == 1 and \
+                        mutant['footer'][0].get('failedAssertions', 0) > 0 and \
+                        set(mutant['assertions']).intersection(mutation['assertions']):
+                    row['status'] = 'killed'
+                elif 'reason' not in row:
+                    row['reason'] = 'No completed, named assertion failure; environment/fixture/timeouts do not kill.'
+            statuses = [x['status'] for x in report['mutations']]
+            report['status'] = 'passed' if all(x == 'killed' for x in statuses) else 'blocked' if 'blocked' in statuses else 'failed'
+            exit_code = 0 if report['status'] == 'passed' else 2 if report['status'] == 'blocked' else 1
+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+        report['reason'] = type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error)
+        report['status'] = 'blocked'
+        exit_code = 2
+    except KeyboardInterrupt:
+        report['reason'] = 'Interrupted; incomplete cleanup or execution cannot pass.'
+        report['status'] = 'blocked'
+        exit_code = 2
+    finally:
+        (output / 'receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
+        print(json.dumps({'status': report['status'], 'receipt': str(output / 'receipt.json')}, ensure_ascii=False))
+    return exit_code
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    raise SystemExit(main())

new file mode 100644

+{
+  "schema": "geometry-browser-mutations/1",
+  "suite": "grid-card-entry-353",
+  "scope": "GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_ONLY",
+  "caseFilter": "GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE",
+  "resultPrefix": "P353_CASE_RESULT ",
+  "cases": [
+    "entry",
+    "explicit_mapping",
+    "commit_cancel",
+    "swap",
+    "equal_swap",
+    "reopen",
+    "native_rejection",
+    "raw_roundtrip",
+    "archive_roundtrip",
+    "history",
+    "lost_source",
+    "stale_preview",
+    "binding_effective"
+  ],
+  "inputs": [
+    "geometry-web/cdp_test.js",
+    "geometry-web/grid_card_entry_test.js",
+    "geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py",
+    "geometry-web/grid_card_entry_mutations.json"
+  ],
+  "mutations": [
+    {
+      "id": "p353_hide_entry",
+      "case": "entry",
+      "assertions": [
+        "ENTRY_CARD_VISIBLE"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js",
+          "before": "<div class=\"ig-card-ref\" id=\"igCardRef\">",
+          "after": "<div class=\"ig-card-ref\" id=\"igCardRef\" style=\"display:none\">"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "p353_swap_noop",
+      "case": "swap",
+      "assertions": [
+        "SWAP_MAPPING_ONLY"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js",
+          "before": "const u = $('igCardU').value; $('igCardU').value = $('igCardV').value; $('igCardV').value = u;",
+          "after": "const u = $('igCardU').value; $('igCardU').value = u; $('igCardV').value = $('igCardV').value;"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "p353_flatten_card_token",
+      "case": "commit_cancel",
+      "assertions": [
+        "CREATE_NATIVE_PREVIEW",
+        "CREATE_PREVIEW_VALID"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js",
+          "before": "if(v.cardLength&&key===(v.source==='repeat'?'step':'panel'))return 'card';",
+          "after": "if(v.cardLength&&key===(v.source==='repeat'?'step':'panel'))return v[key];"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "p353_drop_raw_mapping",
+      "case": "raw_roundtrip",
+      "assertions": [
+        "RAW_SOURCE_MAPPING"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js",
+          "before": "if(s.mode==='source'&&cardOn())for(const id of CARD_IDS)form[id]=$(id)?.value??'';",
+          "after": "if(false&&s.mode==='source'&&cardOn())for(const id of CARD_IDS)form[id]=$(id)?.value??'';"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "p353_drop_card_pins",
+      "case": "raw_roundtrip",
+      "assertions": [
+        "RAW_TYPED_PINS"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js",
+          "before": "if (pin) { s.inputPins.set(key, copy(pin)); s.cardPinKeys.push(key); }",
+          "after": "if (pin) { s.cardPinKeys.push(key); }"
+        }
+      ]
+    }
+  ]
+}

     {
       "path": "geometry-core/framing-template/oracle/selftest.py",
       "reason": "Framing-template oracle self-test selftest.py: mutation, golden-comparison, hand-computed-control and room-sweep suite for framing_oracle, run by tests/run_all.py as the oracle:framing-selftest suite; imports framing_oracle, make_golden, quote_check and the standard library; dedicated test driver, not a product-loaded module or current product entry point.",
```

增量補充與門檻校正。完整 head：`6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2`，base：`405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676`。本輪核對兩個 commit、完整差異、全文討論與相關 SSOT；核對期間已出現[同 head 審查 6439780849](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6439780849)，以下只補未涵蓋的反例，並校正其中 R353-07／08 的範圍。

## 阻擋

- **R353-10／P1｜只改一軸映射，會默默重新取得另一軸的過期來源。** [grid-system-workspace.js:506–509](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L506-L509) 每次呼叫 [cardPins:627–637](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L627-L637)，用目前 catalog 覆寫雙欄 pins。反例：新建草稿保存 A.u=f3、A.v=f8；f8 改值後重開為過期，只把 u 改為 f9，未碰的 v 也換成目前 fingerprint，沒有新舊引用確認。違反 [R54:2502–2503](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/公理層.md#L2502-L2503)。最小修法：未變來源保留原 pin；指紋不符經既有明示確認後才重新取得，取消保留 raw／pins。
- **R353-11／P2｜移除逐欄引用會刪掉整卡仍使用的共享 pin。** [selectParameterPin:94–101](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L94-L101) 只計 `parameterSelections`。反例：整卡 u 與 u.phase 都引用 A.f3；把 phase 改直接值，f3 pin 被刪，整卡映射未改，隨後 [validateDraftPins:143–144](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L143-L144) 卻拒絕合法預覽。最小修法：統一計算逐欄與整卡對 pin 的使用關係，最後一個使用者離開才刪；不要自動重釘來掩蓋。
- **R353-12／P2｜R353-05 尚未完整修好：lost 整卡改指已被其他欄位使用的 B 卡仍被誤拒。** [reference-workspace.js:481–492](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/reference-workspace.js#L481-L492) 不辨識 igCard/U/V，fallback 又要求替代 pin 原本不存在。反例：A.f3/f8 已 lost，B.f3/f8 已分別供兩軸 phase 使用；明選整卡 B 及雙欄後，移除 A lost pins，但 B pins 不是新加入，遂拋「不得純移除」。[D57:1023–1024](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/公理層.md#L1023-L1024)允許明選另一物件。最小修法：按原整卡與新映射驗雙欄原子替換，不以去重後 pin 是否新出現代替引用關係；同卡／同 id 重建仍拒絕。
- **R353-13／P1｜合法「整卡＋來源子規則」候選在確認框出現前拋錯。** [reference-ui.js:203–205](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/reference-ui.js#L203-L205) 仍用 `BigInt(d.step)`；[bridge:16–25、36–50](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/grid_system_bridge.inc#L16-L50)對整卡只輸出 effectiveStep，不輸出 step。真實形狀為整卡 18×30、無錯縫 REPEAT、divide 2×2 時，這裡拋 `TypeError: Cannot convert undefined to a BigInt`，確認框尚未掛載。這是合法組合：[既有 CR16:282–304](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/tests/test_grid_card_reference.py#L282-L304)已有整卡加來源規則與來源改值保留規則的案例。最小修法：摘要讀同一 before／after 的原生有效長度（不可讀 live World 或改寫定義）；回歸應顯示 9×15，並保留一般 direct-step 控制組。

## 須改

- **R353-14／P2｜確認摘要仍無法區分同名同值的不同來源。** [reference-ui.js:184–192](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/reference-ui.js#L184-L192)只顯示卡名與欄名；兩卡同名、欄名及值相同時，換卡前後文字完全相同。選項加了 id 沒有修到此摘要。依 [R60:2571–2572](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/公理層.md#L2571-L2572)，請於兩側同時列 parameterId、uFieldId、vFieldId，讓被替換的來源可辨識。

## 記錄與門檻校正

- R353-01／02／03／04 的主要修法與 06 的 `def()` 修正已在 source 可見；05 只能算部分修正，另有上列共用 consumer 缺口。這些判斷不等於完整 UI 驗收通過。
- **校正 R353-07：相容策略仍須證實，但不能以缺固定 /3 或 /19 號碼單獨判失敗。** [#249 原完整稿](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/issues/249#issuecomment-6435516955)開頭明示新增 schema／UI 是可審閱工程補充，未宣告核可；R60 明定完整專案格式於實作時分配。舊 reader 的[有限 raw 白名單](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676/geometry-web/feature-attachments.js#L234-L236)已有拒絕路徑，尚不能斷言無法拒絕。仍需新 raw 的版本／能力或等效可驗相容措施、舊檔原樣讀取、新檔重開及舊 reader 拒絕且現案不變的證據，不能無聲重算歷史 pins。
- **校正 R353-08：依實際同候選、零寫入及原子歷史判斷，不強制更換 transport。** 原設計警告的是 engineering token 不可交給 line confirm；目前使用 line preview→line confirm 本身不是混用 token 的證據。若有取消別人的候選、錯 owner 消費收據或多一步 Undo，應交具體反例，不能只由函式名稱推得違規。

## 【ChatGPT 實作】PR #353：R353-13／14 確認摘要最小修補

承接[目前 head 的增量審查](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6439972059)，本則交付產品補丁及可重現的函式層核對程式。只修改 `geometry-web/reference-ui.js` 的兩行，2 行新增／2 行刪除；不增加可見 UI 入口。

### 修正內容

1. **R353-13：整卡＋來源子規則的確認摘要拋錯。** 小格尺寸改讀同一側原生候選 row 的 `step ?? effectiveStep`。直接值與逐欄參數仍讀 `step`；整卡讀 `effectiveStep`，各自保留 before／after。原碼整卡 row 沒有 step，會在掛上確認框前拋 `Cannot convert undefined to a BigInt`。輸出契約見 [grid_system_bridge.inc:16–27](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/geometry-web/grid_system_bridge.inc#L16-L27)；合法整卡來源規則見 [CR16](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2/tests/test_grid_card_reference.py#L282-L304)。
2. **R353-14：同名同值的不同來源無法辨認。** 確認摘要在兩側各自列出 parameterId、uFieldId、vFieldId，名稱只作易讀標籤；找不到名稱仍保留三個 ID，符合 [R60 的明示來源及映射](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676/公理層.md#L2570-L2572)。

保留既有 `preview.valid` 與雙軸 REPEAT 的摘要守門。REPEAT_PANEL 原本已以有效 panel＋gap 顯示 pitch，本補丁不把它擴成可用來源子規則的模式。

```diff
--- a/geometry-web/reference-ui.js

     if(!r)return '';
     const card=((typeof oc!=='undefined'&&oc.parameters)||[]).map(o=>o.card).find(c=>c?.id===r.parameterId),name=id=>card?.fields?.find(f=>f.id===id)?.name??`欄位 ${id}`;
-    return `；長度來自整張參數卡「${card?.name??'#'+r.parameterId}」（u = ${name(r.uFieldId)}、v = ${name(r.vFieldId)}）`;

   }
   function source(g) {return g ? `「${g.name}」；${g.mainAlong ? '主支沿 '+g.mainAlong : '主支方向未設定'}；u ${axis(g.uDefinition)}；v ${axis(g.vDefinition)}${cardText(g.cardReference)}` : '不存在';}
   section(sources,'網格來源','system',(item,row)=>{
@@ -201,7 +201,7 @@

-          const sizes=[value.uDefinition,value.vDefinition].map((d,index)=>BigInt(d.step)/BigInt(rule.divisions[index]));
```

### 已執行的局部核對

- 固定原件 Git blob 核對為 `bf4d75d8e232a8743d4c512ab53b1ba61cbea406`。
- 隔離暫存副本的 `git apply --check`、`git apply`、`node --check` 均 exit 0；套用後 bytes 與實際受測版本完全相同，受測新 blob 為 `05886e9e4151ddfb89a00378061dc70fef75f6a1`。
- 環境：Linux／Node v24.22.1；直接執行固定來源的 `appendGridDefinitionImpact` 與真 `gridSourceDefinition`，DOM 使用最小替身。
- **修前：12 案中 7 過、5 失敗，exit 1。修後：12 案全過，exit 0。** 原失敗為 R13_CARD 與四個 R14 來源辨識案例；其餘七個控制組修前／修後都通過。
- 整卡 before 12×30、after 18×30、divide 2×2 分別顯示 6×15／9×15；catalog 故意放 999，摘要仍以候選為準。直接 step、逐欄 ParameterRef、無規則、無效候選及三種 panel 來源均保留原結果；同值對調使用 18×18 的可成立快照。
- 各次核對保留 preview／catalog 原樣；以上是函式層證據，未執行 App／CDP、原生交易、真 Save/Open、手機或最終整合 gate。

### 可重現核對程式（存至暫存目錄，不加入產品檔案）

下列程式從實際 checkout 讀取兩個來源檔，不複製或重寫待驗函式。請保存為暫存 `check-r353-summary.cjs`：

```js
'use strict';
// Function-level regression probe; the DOM below is a test double, not a browser.
const fs=require('node:fs'),vm=require('node:vm'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const ui=fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2]||'geometry-web/reference-ui.js','utf8');
const features=fs.readFileSync(process.argv[3]||'geometry-web/feature-attachments.js','utf8');
const uiStart=ui.indexOf('function appendGridDefinitionImpact(');
const uiEnd=ui.indexOf('function referenceGuidePreparation(',uiStart);
const featureStart=features.indexOf('  const gridSourceDefinition =');
const featureEnd=features.indexOf('  const gridSourceKey =',featureStart);
assert(uiStart>=0&&uiEnd>uiStart&&featureStart>=0&&featureEnd>featureStart,'source functions must exist');
const definition=vm.runInNewContext(features.slice(featureStart,featureEnd)+'\ngridSourceDefinition;');
class Element {
  constructor(tag){this.tagName=tag;this.children=[];this.dataset={};this.attributes={};this.text='';}
  set textContent(value){this.text=String(value);this.children=[];}
  get textContent(){return this.text+this.children.map(n=>n.textContent).join('');}
  append(...nodes){this.children.push(...nodes);}
  setAttribute(name,value){this.attributes[name]=String(value);}
}
const walk=node=>[node,...node.children.flatMap(walk)];
const catalog={parameters:[10,20].map(id=>({card:{id,name:'同名卡',fields:[
  {id:3,name:'同名欄',value:999},{id:8,name:'同名欄',value:999}
]}}))};
const rule={ruleKey:1,name:'二等分',selector:{kind:'all-cells'},divisions:[2,2]};
function grid(kind,u,v,{id=10,swap=false,rules=true,panel=false}={}){
  const fieldIds=swap?[8,3]:[3,8];
  const axis=(length,index)=>{
    const d=panel?{source:'repeat-panel',gap:3,anchor:0}:{source:'repeat',phase:0};
    const key=panel?'panel':'step';
    if(kind==='card'){d[key+'Source']='card';d[panel?'effectivePanel':'effectiveStep']=length;}
    else {d[key]=length;if(kind==='field')d.parameterRefs={[key]:{kind:'parameter',parameterId:id,fieldId:fieldIds[index]}};}
    return d;
  };
  return {id:4,mode:'infinite',name:'來源',mainAlong:null,
    uDefinition:axis(u,0),vDefinition:axis(v,1),nextRuleKey:rules?2:1,rules:rules?[rule]:[],
    ...(kind==='card'?{cardReference:{parameterId:id,uFieldId:fieldIds[0],vFieldId:fieldIds[1]}}:{})};
}
function render(before,after,{valid=true,live=catalog}={}){
  const preview={valid,gridSystems:[{id:4,before,after}]};
  const saved=JSON.stringify(preview),savedLive=JSON.stringify(live);
  const context=vm.createContext({document:{createElement:tag=>new Element(tag)},GeometryProject:{Features:{gridSourceDefinition:definition}},oc:live});
  const append=vm.runInContext(ui.slice(uiStart,uiEnd)+'\nappendGridDefinitionImpact;',context);
  const parent=new Element('dialog');
  append(parent,preview);
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(preview),saved,'render must not mutate the native candidate');
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(live),savedLive,'render must not mutate the live catalog');
  const nodes=walk(parent),side=name=>nodes.find(n=>n.dataset.impactSide===name)?.textContent||'';
  const rules=name=>nodes.find(n=>n.dataset.impactRuleSide===name)?.textContent||'';
  return {before:side('before'),after:side('after'),beforeRules:rules('before'),afterRules:rules('after'),text:parent.textContent};
}
const cases=[];
const add=(id,run)=>cases.push({id,run});
for(const kind of ['card','direct','field'])add('R13_'+kind.toUpperCase(),()=>{
  const r=render(grid(kind,12,30),grid(kind,18,30));
  assert(r.beforeRules.includes('6×15 mm 小格'),'old candidate must display 6 x 15');
  assert(r.afterRules.includes('9×15 mm 小格'),'new candidate must display 9 x 15');
  assert(!r.text.includes('999'),'candidate sizes must not come from the live catalog');
});
add('INVALID_CANDIDATE',()=>{
  const r=render(grid('card',12,30),grid('card',18,30),{valid:false});
  assert(!r.text.includes(' mm 小格'),'invalid candidate must not claim accepted subdivision sizes');
});
add('NO_RULE',()=>{
  const r=render(grid('card',12,30,{rules:false}),grid('card',18,30,{rules:false}));
  assert(!r.text.includes(' mm 小格'));
});
for(const kind of ['card','direct','field'])add('PANEL_'+kind.toUpperCase(),()=>{
  const r=render(grid(kind,18,30,{panel:true,rules:false}),grid(kind,30,30,{panel:true,rules:false}));
  assert(r.before.includes('間距 21 mm'),'panel pitch includes its gap');
  assert(r.after.includes('間距 33 mm'),'new panel pitch uses the new candidate');
  assert(!r.text.includes(' mm 小格'),'panel fixture must not gain source subdivision rules');
});
add('R14_SAME_NAMES',()=>{
  const r=render(grid('card',18,18,{id:10,rules:false}),grid('card',18,18,{id:20,rules:false}));
  assert(r.before.includes('卡 #10')&&r.after.includes('卡 #20'),'same-name cards must expose different ids');
  for(const text of [r.before,r.after])assert(text.includes('u = 同名欄（欄 #3）')&&text.includes('v = 同名欄（欄 #8）'),'both field ids must be visible');
});
add('R14_SAME_VALUES_SWAP',()=>{
  const r=render(grid('card',18,18,{rules:false}),grid('card',18,18,{swap:true,rules:false}));
  assert(r.before.includes('u = 同名欄（欄 #3）')&&r.before.includes('v = 同名欄（欄 #8）'));
  assert(r.after.includes('u = 同名欄（欄 #8）')&&r.after.includes('v = 同名欄（欄 #3）'));
});
for(const [label,live] of [['MISSING_NAMES',{}],['NO_OC',undefined]])add('R14_'+label,()=>{
  // Use a wrapper to preserve an explicitly undefined oc instead of a default.
  const before=grid('card',18,18,{id:10,rules:false}),after=grid('card',18,18,{id:20,rules:false});
  const effectiveLive=live===undefined?null:live;
  if(effectiveLive===null){
    // The production typeof-guard supports an absent oc binding too.
    const context=vm.createContext({document:{createElement:tag=>new Element(tag)},GeometryProject:{Features:{gridSourceDefinition:definition}}});
    const append=vm.runInContext(ui.slice(uiStart,uiEnd)+'\nappendGridDefinitionImpact;',context);
    const parent=new Element('dialog'),preview={valid:true,gridSystems:[{id:4,before,after}]},saved=JSON.stringify(preview);
    append(parent,preview);
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(preview),saved);
    const nodes=walk(parent);
    for(const [side,id] of [['before',10],['after',20]]){
      const text=nodes.find(n=>n.dataset.impactSide===side).textContent;
      assert(text.includes('卡 #'+id)&&text.includes('欄 #3')&&text.includes('欄 #8'),'absent catalog must not hide ids');
    }
  }else{
    const r=render(before,after,{live:effectiveLive});
    assert(r.before.includes('卡 #10')&&r.after.includes('卡 #20'));
    for(const text of [r.before,r.after])assert(text.includes('欄 #3')&&text.includes('欄 #8'));
  }
});
let failed=0;
for(const item of cases){
  try{item.run();process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({case:item.id,status:'PASS'})+'\n');}
  catch(error){failed++;process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({case:item.id,status:'FAIL',error:error.message})+'\n');}
}
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({cases:cases.length,passed:cases.length-failed,failed,scope:'function-level DOM double; no App/native/CDP execution'})+'\n');
process.exitCode=failed?1:0;
```

在 checkout 根目錄執行（將暫存程式路徑換為實際路徑）：

```sh
node /tmp/check-r353-summary.cjs geometry-web/reference-ui.js geometry-web/feature-attachments.js
```

基底預期 `{"cases":12,"passed":7,"failed":5}`／exit 1；套用本補丁後預期 `{"cases":12,"passed":12,"failed":0}`／exit 0。這支 probe 只用於精確重現，本 PR 的既有瀏覽器驗收與突變門檻仍照原交付執行。

### 我無法執行，請驗證：

1. **實際分支套用：** 核對完整基底 SHA 與工作樹，先 `git apply --check` 再套用；`node --check geometry-web/reference-ui.js` 應 exit 0。此補丁只動 reference-ui.js，先前三則瀏覽器測試補丁沒有在本則重交。
2. **真確認框：** 從 App 對整卡 REPEAT 網格預覽合法 2×2 來源子規則；確認框應掛載且 console 無該 BigInt TypeError，原／候選小格尺寸各自正確。保留直接 step 與逐欄 ParameterRef 控制組。

5. **剩餘缺口保持列管：** R353-10／11／12、GEO-219、草稿相容、架構及本片適用驗收仍待處理；本補丁完成不等於整張 PR 可合併。最終來源沿原驗收及已採納的證據沿用規則核收，不為這兩行修改一律重跑無關能力。

## 2. 真 App 實測（head 037a2826）

環境：`geometry-web/start.ps1 -Port 8772`，服務版本 `d1cfd563220d`，bridge 走 WSL。已確認瀏覽器實際載入的 `/reference-ui.js` 含兩處修改。視窗 1400×900；第 3 項另測 390×844。

測試場景：兩張參數卡都叫「同名卡」（#1、#2），各有「長」#2、「寬」#3，四個值都是 600 mm。整卡網格參照卡 #1（u=#2、v=#3），帶一條來源子規則「二等分」（all-cells、divide 2 2）。先用參數面板把卡 #1 的長改成 1200，再做下列操作。

**項目 2：確認框（R353-13）**

在完整工作區把整卡改成 #2，明選 u=長 #2、v=寬 #3，按「預覽來源變更」。結果：

- 「確認連動變更」框正常掛載。
- 自掛事件監聽、`read_console_messages` 都沒有任何錯誤或 BigInt TypeError。
- 原：u 1200、v 600（整張參數卡），每一主格為 4 個 **600×300** mm 小格。
- 新：u 600、v 600，每一主格為 4 個 **300×300** mm 小格。
- 兩側各自正確，沒有讀到現行目錄的值。

**項目 3：來源辨識（R353-14）**

- 換卡時，原側顯示「卡 #1；u = 長（欄 #2）、v = 寬（欄 #3）」，新側顯示「卡 #2；…」。兩張卡同名同值，仍辨識得出。
- 同值對調時，原側是「u = 長（欄 #2）、v = 寬（欄 #3）」，新側是「u = 寬（欄 #3）、v = 長（欄 #2）」，欄 ID 的交換看得出來。
- 390×844 下：
  - 確認框寬 356 px，兩側摘要 `scrollWidth == clientWidth`，沒有橫向溢出，整頁也沒有橫向捲動。
  - 框內可縱向捲動。
  - 「取消，保留原狀」「確認套用」都在視窗內，`elementFromPoint` 命中測試通過。

**項目 4：交易行為**

- 取消：原生 `objects` 的網格逐字相同（仍是卡 #1），編輯器草稿保留在 2／2／3。
- 確認：再預覽一次後確認，套用的是同一候選：卡 #2、600／600，rule key 1 的 2×2 保留。
- 一次 Undo：回到確認前狀態，逐字相同（卡 #1、1200／600、規則 key 1）。先前那步參數修改沒有被連帶復原。
- 一次 Redo：回到確認後狀態，逐字相同。
- 注意：網格工作區是 modal，主畫面的復原鈕在它底下點不到，要先「返回畫布」才能 Undo。這是現有行為，記錄供參考，不算本補丁問題。
