同一份測試正在 `037a2826` 上重跑，確認兩行修補沒有打破它。結果出來後另發一則。

## 4. 新發現（App 實測時看到，非本補丁引入；建議列入 #353 缺口）

**N1｜整卡網格在物件畫布卡片上顯示 `undefined`。** 載入場景後，畫布上整卡網格的卡片有兩處可見文字：

- 「X 間距 undefined · Y 間距 undefined mm」，來自 `app.js:8195` 的 `pitch`：只讀 `d.step`／`d.panel`。
- 「u：無限重複 · 間距 undefined mm，基準 0 mm」，來自 `app.js:8141`：只讀 `d.step`。

整卡 row 只有 `effectiveStep`／`effectivePanel`，所以兩處都顯示 undefined。同類只讀 `step`／`panel` 的位置還有 `app.js:8150`（畫布格線的 `pitch:d.step`）、`app.js:8412`、`grid-inspector.js:28`。這幾處我沒有逐一觀察到可見症狀，只是靜態列出供修補時一併核對。

**N2｜物件檢查器把整卡網格的間距顯示成空白、可編輯。**

- 選取整卡網格後，`#giUStep`／`#giVStep` 值為空，且 `readOnly=false`。完整工作區則會把長度鎖成唯讀。
- 兩格都填 500 再按「預覽變更」，原生回「變更無法套用」，原文：`line 9: I74 CONTROLLED_TOKEN_REJECT: explicitly unbind the whole card reference first`。「確認套用」為停用狀態。
- 原生端守住了（不會靜默解除整卡參照，原生資料逐字不變），但介面讓使用者走進一個技術性的拒絕。
- 只填一格時，檢查器顯示「v 間距須為 1 至 100000000 mm 的整數。」

**N3｜檢查器的「建立配置」對整卡網格一開始就被擋住。** 剛載入、尚未做任何編輯時，檢查器的建立配置鈕標籤就是「請先預覽並保存來源變更，再建立配置。」，推測是空白間距被當成未保存的修改。因此無法從檢查器用整卡網格建立配置。完整工作區的建立配置路徑**未測**。

**附帶：** 同一張卡內兩個欄位不能同名，原生拒絕為 `I39: invalid parameter block record`。探針的 R14_SAME_VALUES_SWAP 替身用了卡內同名欄，真 App 做不出這個狀態；我改用卡內「長／寬」、跨卡同名同值來測，結論不變。

## 未跑

- 完整專案 Save／Open 往返（整卡＋來源子規則），以及草稿相容：未做。
- 手機／平板實機：未做（只做了 390×844 模擬視窗）。
- native 全套（`tests/run_all.py`）：未跑。
- R353-07～12、GEO-219 減項、架構登記：本則未處理，仍待列管。
- 完整工作區的「建立配置」路徑：未測（見 N3）。

【ChatGPT 審查】PR #353 head 037a282

固定 head：`037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e`；上一輪：`6fde4d7ffcd0aac6c7e0cc8c3ef501bc6cd99cf2`；base：`405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676`。已讀本次推送後的兩則【驗證回報】；核對時未見對本 head 的既有【ChatGPT 審查】。本次增量只有 `reference-ui.js` 兩行替換；以下核銷已修事項、保留未動缺口，並補作者新發現的獨立反例。

## 阻擋

- **R353-10／P1、R353-11／P2、R353-12／P2 維持，未被本次兩行修改處理。** 依[上一輪校正審查](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6439972059)：
  - **10：未變的來源遭默默重釘。** [grid-system-workspace.js:506–509、627–637](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L627-L637)：保存 A.u=f3、A.v=f8，f8 過期後只改 u=f9，未碰的 v pin 也被換成目前 fingerprint。保留未變來源的舊 pin；需要重新取得時依 [R54](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676/公理層.md#L2502-L2504)先明示新舊引用並確認，取消不得改 raw／pins。
  - **11：整卡與逐欄共享 pin 被過早刪除。** [selectParameterPin:94–101](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/grid-system-workspace.js#L94-L101)：整卡 u 與 u.phase 同用 A.f3，把 phase 改直接值會刪掉整卡仍需要的 pin。應統一計算兩種引用的使用關係，最後一個使用者離開才刪，不以自動重釘掩蓋。
  - **12：lost 整卡改指已存在的替代 pin 仍遭誤拒。** [reference-workspace.js:481–492](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/reference-workspace.js#L481-L492)：A.f3/f8 已 lost，B.f3/f8 已供 phase 使用，明選整卡 B 後會因 B pins 不是新加入而被當成純移除。應按舊／新整卡映射核對雙欄原子替換；改成另一物件應成功，同 id 重建仍須拒絕。以上三條原反例都是修後必要回歸。
- **R353-07 的校正範圍及 R353-09 的剩餘部分仍待閉合。** 新回報補上部分瀏覽器與保存重開證據，詳見下方；尚未證實新 raw 的完整相容策略、舊 reader 拒絕且現案不變，以及 committed／raw-only／mixed 的適用矩陣。仍不得無聲重算歷史 pins，但不以缺少固定 `/3` 或 `/19` 號碼單獨判失敗。GEO-219 的實際合併／減項與 BACKLOG 記錄未在本增量變動；本片適用架構核對與最終驗收也不能由 151 個斷言自動核銷。[既有範圍校正](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6439972059)／[GEO-219 規則](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676/AGENTS.md#L279-L285)。

## 須改

- **R353-15／P2｜整卡的正常畫布卡片仍讀 literal 長度，文字錯誤且示意格線消失。** [app.js:8141–8161](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/app.js#L8141-L8161)及 [8195–8196](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/app.js#L8195-L8196)只讀 `step`／`panel`。以原 `GridSystemEditor.card` 函式搭配最小 SVG 替身，literal 600×1200 控制組畫出 24 條格線；同有效尺寸的整卡 repeat/repeat、repeat/panel、panel/panel 均為 0 條，摘要分別出現 `undefined`／`NaN`。原因是有效長度在 `effectiveStep`／`effectivePanel`，使目前 pitch／scale 變成 NaN。最小修法：顯示、pitch、相位位置與縮圖統一讀同一原生 row 的有效長度，保留來源語意；回歸涵蓋三種軸組合及 direct／逐欄控制組。此為函式層獨立重現，與[作者實見的文字錯誤](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6450877142)一致；`app.js:8412` 的 fallback 未作正常路徑實見宣稱。
- **R353-16／P2｜檢查器未認識整卡控制來源，產生假修改並送出錯誤的來源定義。** [grid-inspector.js:57–62、84](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/grid-inspector.js#L57-L84)以 `d.step` 填值與判 dirty，唯讀判斷只看逐欄 ParameterRef。原函式＋空 number 欄位夾具確認：整卡含至少一個 repeat 軸時，尚未編輯就禁用「建立配置」並要求先保存；兩軸 repeat-panel 不經此假 dirty 路徑。另抽取[saveSource:166–186](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/grid-inspector.js#L166-L186)捕獲 preview：把兩個空欄填 500 後，輸出沒有 `card-reference`、兩軸都是 literal `500`；兩軸 panel 連未改長度也會輸出 `repeat-panel undefined`。作者已在真 App 回報 I74 拒絕且正式資料不變，本輪未親驗 native。最小修法須同時處理有效值顯示、受控唯讀、未編輯比較，以及序列化保留整卡 ID／雙欄映射與 `card` token；不能僅補有效數字後把來源拍平成 literal。若此精簡檢查器暫不承接整卡編輯，應明確導向既有完整工作區，仍保留合法「建立配置」入口。回歸需覆蓋未編輯即可建立配置、整卡僅改名保留來源、三種軸組合與既有 direct／逐欄編輯。

## 記錄

1. **R353-13、R353-14 的兩行修碼已確認進入 head，可解除這兩項修碼缺口。** [reference-ui.js:184–205](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/blob/037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e/geometry-web/reference-ui.js#L184-L205)與[交付補丁](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6448189582)一致；下載原件重新計算的 Git blob 為 `05886e9e4151ddfb89a00378061dc70fef75f6a1`，舊版為 `bf4d75d8e232a8743d4c512ab53b1ba61cbea406`。我獨立執行 `node --check` 為 exit 0，原附函式 probe 舊版 7/12、新版 12/12；before 12×30 與 after 18×30 的 2×2 小格各為 6×15、9×15，不讀 catalog 的 999。
2. **承認並處理原 probe 的名稱夾具限制。** 同卡兩欄同名不是可成立的原生輸入；我另把卡內名稱改為「長／寬」、保留跨卡同名同值及對調，並同步修正名稱斷言，結果仍為舊 7/12、新 12/12。這是函式＋DOM 替身核對，不宣稱代替原生合法性或 App 驗收。作者另回報真 App 以長／寬完成摘要、390px、取消、確認及單步 Undo/Redo；列為作者實測證據。[驗證回報](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6450877142)。
3. **收錄 current head 的瀏覽器 v1 回報：13 案、151 過／0 敗、5 個突變 killed，runner exit 0。** [6451077103](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6451077103)給定 head 037a282、另套測試補丁的 staged diff 短雜湊 `aca23db1dc3d7d30`、WSL／Chrome 與命令。我從三則交付補丁還原 test driver，其 LF SHA-256 `31639687991d2f6dc9bd687601728f78981ef06287e5e40a1431e7ee88cab41e` 與補丁 manifest 一致；但回報未附 `receipt.json`、逐案 `P353_CASE_RESULT`／`CDP_RESULT` 或各 run 原始日誌，故沒有獨立核實受測來源與逐次收據。這批測試是額外套用的留言補丁，本次 head 的兩行修改沒有納入它。
4. **v1 的 raw_roundtrip／archive_roundtrip 確實包含正式下載與 Open，不能再籠統說沒有 Save/Open 證據。** 接受作者已跑這兩條的回報；未涵蓋的是整卡＋來源子規則往返及上述新 raw 相容矩陣。`lost_source` 測的是同 id 重建拒絕、非 lost 草稿改另一卡；`stale_preview` 測的是預覽後 World 競爭。它們未建立 R353-10／11／12 的反例前提，151／5 不代表這三條已修。
5. **R353-08 沿用校正，不恢復原先過大的要求。** 依同候選、取消／失敗／過期零寫入與原子歷史判斷；目前 line preview→line confirm 不因名稱就構成 token 混用，也不強制換 transport。整組解除可按已採納分片留後續，本片不得暗解除或宣稱整套 UI 完成。新 App／v1 回報可作相應交易案例證據。

本輪未親自執行 App／CDP、WSL runner、native 全套、完整專案整卡＋來源子規則往返、舊 reader 相容矩陣、實體手機或最終架構 gate；不擴大重跑未變能力。R353-10～12 沿用前次固定來源反例，本次以增量確認相關程式未動；新親驗範圍為摘要 probe、畫布／檢查器原函式夾具及 preview 命令捕獲。

## 結論

**R353-13／14 已修；仍不建議合併。** 優先修 R353-10／11／12 與本次確認的 R353-15／16，保留已校正的 R353-07／09 剩餘事項。新回報有實質進展，應按實際案例收錄；不把作者回報升格為本輪親驗，也不把既有 13 案擴張成未測反例的通過證明。

## 1. 收據 `files` 的 25,002 筆，全部對得回來源

我從本機 repo 取 `037a2826` 的 tree，對每個 blob 算 SHA-256（git 儲存內容，即 LF），再逐筆比對收據 `files`：

|---|---|
| head tree 的 blob | 24,999 |
| 收據 `files` | 25,002 |
| 兩邊都有且雜湊相同 | 24,997 |
| 兩邊都有但雜湊不同 | 2：`geometry-web/cdp_test.js`、`geometry-core/domain-decoupling/product-architecture.json` |
| 只在收據 | 3：`geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py`、`geometry-web/grid_card_entry_mutations.json`、`geometry-web/grid_card_entry_test.js` |
| 只在 tree | 0 |

這 5 個檔正好是留言測試補丁 `pr353-tests-v1.patch`（SHA-256 `722e207fb89d8400adeefb1b3b0f257018fdfec1e806233644c5ed917b4a8e33`）動到的 5 個檔。

我另用暫時 index（不碰工作目錄）做了一次：`git read-tree 037a2826` → `git apply --cached` 套上這份補丁 → 取出 5 個檔重算雜湊。5 筆都與收據相同：

| 檔案 | SHA-256 |
|---|---|
| `browser_mutation_check.py` | `7ad42d2e…` |
| `grid_card_entry_mutations.json` | `dfcd555e…`（也就是 `manifestHash`） |
| `grid_card_entry_test.js` | `31639687…` |
| `product-architecture.json` | `3bb0a539…` |
| `cdp_test.js` | `9326f83a…` |

`sourceHash`：runner 第 427 行的算法是 `digest(json.dumps(report['files'], sort_keys=True).encode())`。我依此重算得 `feacdb4e…db5cb`，與收據相同。

結論：受測來源 = head `037a2826` 原樣 + 這份測試補丁，沒有其他檔。

## 2. native binary 與來源的對應

`bridgeHash`、`modelBaseHash` 是 runner 對 `--bridge`、`--model-base` 兩個檔的 SHA-256（第 417、419 行）。這兩個檔由驗證腳本在同一份 checkout 內用以下指令編出：

```text
gcc -std=c11 -O1 geometry-web/web_bridge.c -lm -o web_bridge
gcc -std=c11 -O1 geometry-core/model_base.c -lm -o model-base
```

| 建置 | web_bridge | model-base |
|---|---|---|
| 收據記錄 | `3f085773…806bf83` | `c0ab5d1b…b94422` |
| 當次 run 留下的執行檔，重算 | 相同 | 相同 |
| 全新 clone `037a2826` → `git apply --index` 補丁 → 同指令重編（gcc 13.3.0，路徑不同） | 相同 | 相同 |
| 對照：main `405f8c5e` 同指令 | 相同 | 相同 |
| 對照：#354 head `b5b33ed1` 同指令 | `e50e6b79…` 不同 | `336d35bb…` 不同 |

兩個對照組的解讀：
- main 編出相同雜湊，是因為 `037a2826` 與 main 的 `*.c`／`*.h`／`*.inc` 差異是 0 個檔。#353 只改網頁層，native 與 main 位元相同。
- `b5b33ed1` 有 7 個 C 系檔不同，雜湊就不同。所以這個比對確實會隨 C 來源改變。

結論：收據的兩個 binary，可由 head 原始碼加同一組指令逐位元重現。

|---|---|---|
| R353-10 | `pin_preserve_untouched` | A.u=f3、A.v=f8；f8 過期後只改 u→f9，v 的 pin 與 raw 長度不變，過期預覽拒絕且沒有額外寫入／歷史 |
| R353-10 | `pin_reacquire_cancel`、`pin_reacquire_confirm` | 明選 stale 共享欄位，核對確認訊息的 before／after；取消全保留，接受後才取得新 pin，再以合法雙欄提交 |
| R353-11 | `pin_shared_consumers` | 先移除 phase 或先移除整卡映射都保留另一使用者的 pin；最後一個離開才刪，stale pin 不得偷偷更新 |
| R353-12 | `lost_card_existing_pins` | B.f3/f8 已供兩軸 phase 使用；選卡及只選一軸時不部分寫入，完整映射原子替換 lost A |
| R353-12 | `lost_card_same_id_rebuild` | 同 ID 重建後，暫選 B 再選回 A 仍須拒絕；保留 lost 證據與正式場景 |
| R353-15 | `card_render_repeat_repeat`、`card_render_repeat_panel`、`card_render_panel_panel` | 整卡、direct、逐欄同值控制組；核對有效文字、pitch、實際 SVG 格線與相位位置，不能只檢查「有線」 |
| R353-16 | `inspector_repeat_repeat`、`inspector_repeat_panel`、`inspector_panel_panel` | 有效值、受控唯讀、無假 dirty、未編輯建立配置、只改名的取消／提交、整卡 ID／雙欄映射／card token |
| 控制組 | `inspector_direct_control`、`inspector_parameter_control` | 直接值實際改為 650；逐欄改名保留 param token；混合來源仍可編輯直接值軸 |

Mutation manifest 保留原 5 個突變，新增 9 個，共 14 個。原 `p353_drop_card_pins` 僅把 needle 移到新實作的等義「不加入整卡 pin」位置，沒有移除其斷言。新增突變覆蓋未變軸重釘、漏算共享使用者、略過確認、誤拒既有 B pins、畫布 literal 長度，以及檢查器唯讀／dirty／card token／mapping。

我已核對 14 個 mutation 的 exact needle 各命中一次、每個替換後 JS 均可通過語法檢查；這是靜態與語法核對。真 browser mutation runner 尚未執行，不能將它寫成「14 個 killed」。函式層實際撤修結果見 3/3。

### 只套 T、不套 P：CDP 預期失敗案例

以下 12 案為依原碼與函式層證據推論的 CDP 紅對照，**本環境未執行 CDP**：

```text
pin_preserve_untouched
pin_reacquire_cancel
pin_reacquire_confirm
pin_shared_consumers
lost_card_existing_pins
lost_card_same_id_rebuild
card_render_repeat_repeat
card_render_repeat_panel
card_render_panel_panel
inspector_repeat_repeat
inspector_repeat_panel
inspector_panel_panel
```

`lost_card_same_id_rebuild` 多了「暫選 B 再回 A」前提，所以也在紅名單；原 v1 的直接同 ID 重建拒絕案例 `lost_source` 保留。新增 direct／逐欄 inspector 兩案是應綠控制組。

先從兩則留言取回 T 的兩段，再依下列順序執行：

```bash
cat pr353-T.part1.patch pr353-T.part2.patch > pr353-T.patch
sha256sum pr353-T.patch
git apply --check pr353-T.patch
git apply pr353-T.patch
node tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js
node tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js
```

這個階段兩支函式測試預期各為 exit 1，失敗名稱及我親跑的結果見 2/3。沿用本機的真 bridge 測試服務，以及 `GEOMETRY_TEST_URL`、`GEOMETRY_FEEDBACK_DIR` 等既有環境設定，再跑 CDP 紅對照：

```bash
GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_ONLY=1 node geometry-web/cdp_test.js
```

需要單案可另設 `GEOMETRY_GRID_CARD_ENTRY_CASE=pin_preserve_untouched` 等具名案例。**完整 mutation runner 要等套 P 後才跑**，因新撤修 needles 是修後原碼的位置；T-only 階段直接跑 driver／函式測試。

### 我親自在函式層執行的結果

Node `v24.22.1`。在乾淨的指定基底＋tests-v1 檔案上，先實際 `git apply T`，執行紅對照，再實際 `git apply P`，執行綠對照。兩份補丁的 `git apply --check --whitespace=error-all` 都成功，套完後所有改動檔逐位元等於交付原件。

| 測試 | 只套 T、不套 P | T＋P |
|---|---:|---:|
| `test_pr353_pin_regressions.js` | 4 過／10 失敗，exit 1 | 14 過／0 失敗，exit 0 |
| `test_pr353_display_regressions.js` | 13 過／11 失敗，exit 1 | 24 過／0 失敗，exit 0 |
| 合計 | **17 過／21 失敗** | **38／38** |

這些執行原產品函式，pins 套件包含原 card／dialog 事件 handler、`syncCard`、`cardPins`、`selectParameterPin`、`flush`、`validateDraftPins`、`setGridDraft`，以及真的 Features／schema validator／ZIP writer、reader、ReferenceWorkspace local Undo／Redo。畫面套件執行原 `GridSystemEditor.card`、完整 `grid-inspector.js` 與原 source-key 計算。

DOM／SVG、事件派送、native `objects` 與 preview 傳輸使用明示的替身；裝飾性重畫及 `syncDraftState` 的呈現 wrapper 被隔離，保留真 `flush`。因此以上是**函式層證據**，不是實際 App、native preview／confirm、瀏覽器 Save/Open 或完整 CDP 驗收。

R353-12 另外有直接呼叫真 `setGridDraft` 的案例，獨立證明既有 B pins 誤拒，避免被更早的 UI input 問題遮住。R353-10 包含 `input → change` 取消，以及 swap 第一次確認接受、第二次取消時 raw／pins／歷史全部不變。

### 只套 T 時，親自觀察到的失敗名稱

Pins：

```text
R353-10 unchanged V mapping retains stale fingerprint and raw; preview rejects
R353-10 input then change cancellation preserves all raw pins and history
R353-10 explicit stale shared-field selection shows old/new pins before confirmation
R353-10 swap second confirmation cancellation rolls back both axes atomically
R353-11 phase unlink retains the exact pin still consumed by the whole card
R353-11 phase unlink preserves a stale whole-card pin without automatic re-acquisition
R353-11 either consumer may leave first; only the last user releases the pin
R353-12 direct atomic draft replacement accepts B pins already used by both phases
R353-12 card selection reuses both existing phase pins with atomic history and ZIP roundtrip
R353-12 same card and same-ID reconstruction remain rejected after an incomplete selection
```

### T-2／2：unified diff

```diff
diff --git a/tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js b/tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..73658ef5f5ebd04e8f3bb70e561f28058e0b91ab
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js
@@ -0,0 +1,129 @@
+/*
+ * PR #353 R353-15/16: FUNCTION-LAYER regression checks, with R353-13/14 controls.
+ * Runs the actual GridSystemEditor.card and grid-inspector.js functions, and
+ * the actual feature-attachments.js source-key implementation. R353-13/14 use
+ * the original appendGridDefinitionImpact summary function. Minimal SVG,
+ * DOM and transport doubles provide the host only; this does not run a browser,
+ * native geometry, preview confirmation, or save/reopen.
+ *
+ * node tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js [repo-root] [mutation]
+ * P353_REPO_ROOT can select a baseline checkout; default is this repo.
+ * Optional rollback mutations (in memory, never written to product files):
+ *   canvas_literal          -> function_R353_15_canvas_{rr,rp,pp} must fail
+ *   inspector_literal       -> function_R353_16_rename_card_{rr,rp,pp} must fail
+ *   inspector_omit_mapping  -> function_R353_16_rename_card_{rr,rp,pp} must fail
+ * A mutation run is expected to exit 1. P353_DISPLAY_MUTATION is also accepted.
+ */
+'use strict';
+const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path'),vm=require('vm'),assert=require('assert/strict');
+const root=path.resolve(process.argv[2]||process.env.P353_REPO_ROOT||path.join(__dirname,'..'));
+const Features=require(path.join(root,'geometry-web/feature-attachments.js'));
+const mutation=process.argv[3]||process.env.P353_DISPLAY_MUTATION||null;
+const mutations={
+  canvas_literal:['app.js',"const axisLength = (d,key) => d?.[key+'Source']==='card' ? d?.[key==='step'?'effectiveStep':'effectivePanel'] : d?.[key];","const axisLength = (d,key) => d?.[key];"],
+  inspector_literal:['grid-inspector.js',"if(d[key+'Source']==='card')return 'card';",''],
+  inspector_omit_mapping:['grid-inspector.js',"${s.object.mainAlong||'none'}${card} ${parts.join(' ')}","${s.object.mainAlong||'none'} ${parts.join(' ')}"]
+};
+if(mutation&&!Object.hasOwn(mutations,mutation))throw Error('Unknown mutation: '+mutation);
+function sourceText(file){const text=fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'geometry-web',file),'utf8'),edit=mutations[mutation];if(!edit||edit[0]!==file)return text;assert.equal(text.split(edit[1]).length-1,1,'mutation needle count');return text.replace(edit[1],edit[2]);}
+const results=[];
+async function check(name,fn){name='function_'+name;try{await fn();results.push({name,status:'pass'});}catch(e){results.push({name,status:'fail',error:e.message});}}
+const escape=value=>String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
+const clone=value=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
+const definitions=(kind,card=false,refs=false)=>{
+  const modes={rr:['repeat','repeat'],rp:['repeat','repeat-panel'],pp:['repeat-panel','repeat-panel']}[kind];
+  return Object.fromEntries(modes.map((source,i)=>{
+    const length=i===0?600:1200,key=source==='repeat'?'step':'panel';
+    const d=source==='repeat'?{source,phase:i===0?-125:37,step:length}:{source,panel:length,gap:i===0?7:5,anchor:i===0?-70:45,anchorExplicit:true};
+    if(card){delete d[key];d[key+'Source']='card';d[key==='step'?'effectiveStep':'effectivePanel']=length;}
+    if(refs)d.parameterRefs={[key]:{kind:'parameter',parameterId:10,fieldId:i===0?3:8}};
+    if(i===0){d.stagger=137;d.parameterRefs={...d.parameterRefs,stagger:{kind:'parameter',parameterId:20,fieldId:1}};}
+    return [(i?'v':'u')+'Definition',d];
+  }));
+};
+const row=(kind,card=false,refs=false)=>({kind:'grid-system',id:41,name:'Grid fixture',mode:'infinite',mainAlong:'u',cx:0,cy:0,applications:[],bindings:[],rules:[],nextRuleKey:1,...definitions(kind,card,refs),...(card?{cardReference:{parameterId:10,uFieldId:3,vFieldId:8}}:{})});
+function canvas(){
+  const text=sourceText('app.js'),start=text.indexOf('const GridSystemEditor = (() => {'),end=text.indexOf('\nfunction renderGridObjectInspector',start);
+  assert(start>=0&&end>start);
+  const el=(tag,attrs={},parent)=>{const n={tag,attrs,children:[],textContent:''};if(parent)parent.children.push(n);return n;};
+  const context=vm.createContext({ReferenceWorkspace:{escape},el,OC_W:260,OC_H:220,TextEncoder,structuredClone});
+  vm.runInContext(text.slice(start,end)+'\nglobalThis.cardProbe=GridSystemEditor.card;',context);
+  const flatten=n=>[n,...n.children.flatMap(flatten)];
+  return o=>{
+    const n=context.cardProbe(o),nodes=flatten(n),body=nodes.find(n=>n.attrs.class==='ocard-body');
+    return {text:nodes.map(n=>n.textContent).join('\n'),lines:flatten(body).filter(n=>n.tag==='line').map(n=>clone(n.attrs)),attributes:nodes.flatMap(n=>Object.values(n.attrs))};
+  };
+}
+class FakeNode {
+  constructor(id,tag,attrs){this.id=id;this.tagName=tag.toUpperCase();this.attrs=attrs;this.value=attrs.value??'';this.disabled=Object.hasOwn(attrs,'disabled');this.readOnly=Object.hasOwn(attrs,'readonly');this.isConnected=true;this.listeners={};this.classList={add(){},remove(){},toggle(){}};this.textContent='';if(attrs.type==='number'&&this.value!==''&&!Number.isFinite(Number(this.value)))this.value='';}
+  addEventListener(type,fn){(this.listeners[type]??=[]).push(fn);}
+  async emit(type){for(const fn of this.listeners[type]||[])await fn({target:this});}
+  setAttribute(key,value){this.attrs[key]=String(value);}
+  focus(){}
+}
+class FakePane {
+  constructor(){this.nodes=new Map();this.isConnected=true;this.classList={add(){},remove(){}};}
+  set innerHTML(html){this.html=html;this.nodes.clear();for(const m of html.matchAll(/<(section|input|button|p|select)\b([^>]*)>/g)){const attrs={};for(const a of m[2].matchAll(/([\w-]+)(?:="([^"]*)")?/g))attrs[a[1]]=a[2]??'';if(attrs.id)this.nodes.set(attrs.id,new FakeNode(attrs.id,m[1],attrs));}}
+  get innerHTML(){return this.html;}
+  querySelector(selector){return selector.startsWith('#')?this.nodes.get(selector.slice(1))??null:null;}
+  querySelectorAll(selector){if(selector==='button,input,select')return [...this.nodes.values()].filter(n=>['BUTTON','INPUT','SELECT'].includes(n.tagName));return selector.split(',').map(s=>this.querySelector(s)).filter(Boolean);}
+}
+function inspector(o){
+  const pane=new FakePane(),contextId={id:'probe'},commands=[],bindings=[];
+  const workspace={createBinding:id=>bindings.push(id),openSystem(){},openBinding(){}},oc={gridSystems:[clone(o)],gridBindings:[],faces:[],bmap:{},busy:false};
+  const context=vm.createContext({window:{InfiniteGridWorkspace:workspace},ReferenceWorkspace:{escape,context:()=>contextId},GeometryProject:{Features},oc,structuredClone,TextEncoder,
+    document:{querySelector:()=>null},deleteObject(){},renderObjects:async()=>{},confirmReferenceImpact:async()=>({ok:false,error:'probe cancelled'}),apiRequest:async command=>{commands.push(command);if(command==='objects')return {ok:true,gridSystems:clone(oc.gridSystems)};if(command.startsWith('line preview '))return {preview:{token:'probe',command}};throw Error('unexpected API '+command);}});
+  const text=sourceText('grid-inspector.js').replace('workspace.renderInspector=renderInspector;workspace.disposeInspector=disposeInspector;','workspace.renderInspector=renderInspector;workspace.disposeInspector=disposeInspector;workspace.probe={saveSource,syncSourceDraft,sourceForm,definitionText,getActive:()=>active};');
+  vm.runInContext(text,context);workspace.renderInspector(pane,clone(o));
+  return {pane,workspace,oc,commands,bindings,state:workspace.probe.getActive()};
+}
+function impactSummary(before,after,cards){
+  const createElement=tag=>({tag,children:[],dataset:{},attrs:{},textContent:'',append(...nodes){this.children.push(...nodes);},setAttribute(key,value){this.attrs[key]=String(value);}});
+  const context=vm.createContext({document:{createElement},GeometryProject:{Features},oc:{parameters:cards.map(card=>({id:card.id,card}))}});
+  const text=sourceText('reference-ui.js'),start=text.indexOf('function appendGridDefinitionImpact('),end=text.indexOf('\nfunction referenceGuidePreparation(',start);
+  assert(start>=0&&end>start);vm.runInContext(text.slice(start,end),context);
+  const preview={valid:true,gridSystems:[{id:41,before,after}]},snapshot=JSON.stringify(preview),dialog=createElement('dialog');
+  context.appendGridDefinitionImpact(dialog,preview);assert.equal(JSON.stringify(preview),snapshot,'summary cannot rewrite candidate definitions');
+  const flatten=n=>[n,...n.children.flatMap(flatten)],nodes=flatten(dialog),textOf=n=>flatten(n).map(x=>x.textContent).join('\n');
+  return {text:textOf(dialog),side:side=>nodes.find(n=>n.dataset.impactSide===side)?.textContent,rules:side=>textOf(nodes.find(n=>n.dataset.impactRuleSide===side))};
+}
+const summaryCard=(id,u=18,v=30)=>({id,name:'同名參數卡',fields:[{id:3,name:'長',type:'dimension',value:{num:String(u),den:'1'}},{id:8,name:'寬',type:'dimension',value:{num:String(v),den:'1'}}]});
+function summaryRow(card,u=18,v=30){
+  const o=row('rr',card);o.uDefinition=card?{source:'repeat',phase:0,stepSource:'card',effectiveStep:u}:{source:'repeat',phase:0,step:u};
+  o.vDefinition=card?{source:'repeat',phase:0,stepSource:'card',effectiveStep:v}:{source:'repeat',phase:0,step:v};return o;
+}
+(async()=>{
+  const draw=canvas();
+  for(const kind of ['rr','rp','pp']){
+    await check('R353_15_canvas_'+kind,()=>{const card=row(kind,true),before=JSON.stringify(card),a=draw(card),b=draw(row(kind));assert(b.lines.length>0);assert.deepEqual(a.lines,b.lines);assert(a.lines.length>0);assert(!/undefined|NaN/.test(a.text));assert(a.attributes.every(v=>!String(v).includes('NaN')));assert(a.text.includes('600'));assert(a.text.includes('1200')||a.text.includes('1205'));assert(a.text.includes('整張參數卡'));assert.equal(JSON.stringify(card),before);});
+    await check('canvas_direct_parameter_'+kind,()=>{const d=draw(row(kind)),p=draw(row(kind,false,true));assert(d.lines.length>0);assert.deepEqual(p.lines,d.lines);assert(!/undefined|NaN/.test(d.text+p.text));});
+    await check('R353_16_effective_readonly_'+kind,()=>{const p=inspector(row(kind,true));assert(!/undefined|NaN/.test(p.pane.html));for(const [axis,length]of[['U','600'],['V','1200']]){const n=p.pane.querySelector('#gi'+axis+'Step');if(n){assert.equal(n.value,length);assert.equal(n.readOnly,true);assert.equal(n.attrs['aria-readonly'],'true');}}assert(p.pane.html.includes('整張參數卡'));});
+    await check('R353_16_clean_binding_'+kind,async()=>{const p=inspector(row(kind,true)),create=p.pane.querySelector('#gridObjectNewBinding');assert.equal(create.disabled,false);await create.emit('click');assert.deepEqual(p.bindings,[41]);assert.equal(p.commands.length,0);});
+    // Before P, invalid number values become empty; fill 500 only to reach the
+    // reviewed flattening counterexample. With P, the readonly effective values remain.
+    await check('R353_16_rename_card_'+kind,async()=>{const o=row(kind,true),p=inspector(o),before=JSON.stringify(p.state.object);p.pane.querySelector('#giName').value='renamed';await p.pane.querySelector('#giName').emit('input');assert.equal(p.pane.querySelector('#gridObjectNewBinding').disabled,true);for(const axis of ['U','V']){const input=p.pane.querySelector('#gi'+axis+'Step');if(input&&!input.value)input.value='500';}await p.pane.querySelector('#giPreview').emit('click');const cmd=p.commands.find(x=>x.startsWith('line preview '));assert(cmd,'preview command was not captured');assert(cmd.includes('card-reference 10 3 8'));assert(!/undefined|NaN/.test(cmd));const expected=kind==='rr'?'u repeat -125 card stagger param:20:1 v repeat 37 card':kind==='rp'?'u repeat -125 card stagger param:20:1 v repeat-panel card gap 5 from 45':'u repeat-panel card gap 7 from -70 stagger param:20:1 v repeat-panel card gap 5 from 45';assert(cmd.endsWith(expected),cmd);assert.equal(JSON.stringify(p.state.object),before);});
+    await check('inspector_direct_parameter_'+kind,async()=>{for(const refs of [false,true]){const o=row(kind,false,refs),p=inspector(o);assert.equal(p.pane.querySelector('#gridObjectNewBinding').disabled,false);for(const axis of ['U','V']){const input=p.pane.querySelector('#gi'+axis+'Step');if(input)assert.equal(input.readOnly,refs);}p.pane.querySelector('#giName').value='renamed';await p.pane.querySelector('#giPreview').emit('click');const cmd=p.commands.find(x=>x.startsWith('line preview '));assert(cmd);assert(!cmd.includes('card-reference'));assert(!/undefined|NaN/.test(cmd));if(refs){assert(cmd.includes('param:10:3'));assert(cmd.includes('param:10:8'));}else{assert(cmd.includes('600'));assert(cmd.includes('1200'));}}});
+  }
+  await check('inspector_direct_edit',async()=>{const p=inspector(row('rr'));p.pane.querySelector('#giUStep').value='650';await p.pane.querySelector('#giUStep').emit('input');assert.equal(p.pane.querySelector('#gridObjectNewBinding').disabled,true);await p.pane.querySelector('#giPreview').emit('click');assert(p.commands.some(x=>x.endsWith('u repeat -125 650 stagger param:20:1 v repeat 37 1200')));});
+  await check('inspector_stale_guard',async()=>{const p=inspector(row('rr',true));p.pane.querySelector('#giName').value='renamed';for(const axis of ['U','V']){const field=p.pane.querySelector('#gi'+axis+'Step');if(field&&!field.value)field.value='500';}p.oc.gridSystems[0].uDefinition.effectiveStep=700;await p.pane.querySelector('#giPreview').emit('click');assert.equal(p.commands.filter(x=>x.startsWith('line preview ')).length,0);assert(p.pane.querySelector('#giStatus').textContent.includes('已更新'));});
+  for(const card of [true,false])await check('R353_13_control_'+(card?'card':'direct')+'_rule_before_after',()=>{
+    const before=summaryRow(card,12,30),after=summaryRow(card,18,30),rule={ruleKey:1,name:'二乘二',selector:{kind:'all-cells'},divisions:[2,2]};
+    for(const o of [before,after]){o.rules=[clone(rule)];o.nextRuleKey=2;}
+    const summary=impactSummary(before,after,[summaryCard(10,999,999)]);
+    assert(summary.rules('before').includes('6×15 mm'));assert(summary.rules('after').includes('9×15 mm'));
+    assert(!/undefined|NaN|999/.test(summary.text),'rule arithmetic must use its own before/after native row');
+  });
+  await check('R353_14_control_equal_labels_distinct_cards',()=>{
+    const before=summaryRow(true),after=clone(before);after.cardReference.parameterId=20;
+    const summary=impactSummary(before,after,[summaryCard(10),summaryCard(20)]),old=summary.side('before'),next=summary.side('after');
+    for(const text of [old,next]){assert(text.includes('同名參數卡'));assert(text.includes('u = 長（欄 #3）'));assert(text.includes('v = 寬（欄 #8）'));}
+    assert(old.includes('卡 #10'));assert(next.includes('卡 #20'));assert.notEqual(old.slice(2),next.slice(2));
+  });
+  await check('R353_14_control_equal_lengths_swapped_fields',()=>{
+    const before=summaryRow(true,18,18),after=clone(before);after.cardReference.uFieldId=8;after.cardReference.vFieldId=3;
+    const summary=impactSummary(before,after,[summaryCard(10,18,18)]),old=summary.side('before'),next=summary.side('after');
+    assert(old.includes('卡 #10'));assert(next.includes('卡 #10'));assert(old.includes('u = 長（欄 #3）'));assert(old.includes('v = 寬（欄 #8）'));
+    assert(next.includes('u = 寬（欄 #8）'));assert(next.includes('v = 長（欄 #3）'));assert.notEqual(old.slice(2),next.slice(2));
+  });
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({schema:'pr353-display-function-tests/1',root,mutation,layer:'original functions with minimal SVG/DOM and transport doubles; no browser/native run',passed:results.filter(r=>r.status==='pass').length,failed:results.filter(r=>r.status==='fail').length,results},null,2));process.exitCode=results.some(r=>r.status==='fail')?1:0;
+})();
diff --git a/tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js b/tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3d236ab23cab09e56eeb7c5f9c99e36ecca820b1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js
@@ -0,0 +1,235 @@
+/* PR353 function-layer regressions. Run with Node 24:
+ *   node tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js [repo-root] [R353-10|R353-11|R353-12]
+ * P353_REPO_ROOT and P353_PIN_MUTATION are equivalent environment overrides.
+ * The real grid handlers, ReferenceWorkspace, Features validator and ZIP writer
+ * execute. DOM/event delivery and native objects responses are explicit doubles;
+ * this is not browser/native E2E or an actual Save/Open session.
+ */
+'use strict';
+const {test}=require('node:test'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
+const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),vm=require('node:vm');
+const root=path.resolve(process.env.P353_REPO_ROOT||process.env.GEOMETRY_PR353_ROOT||process.argv[2]||path.join(__dirname,'..'));
+const mutation=process.env.P353_PIN_MUTATION||process.argv[3]||'';
+const P=require(path.join(root,'geometry-web/project-store.js'));
+const zip=require(path.join(root,'geometry-web/vendor/fflate/umd/index.js'));
+const mutations={
+  'R353-10':{file:'grid-system-workspace.js',before:'if(before&&!selectedIds.includes(id))continue;',after:'if(before&&!selectedIds.includes(id)){if(pin)nextPins.set(key,copy(pin));continue;}'},
+  'R353-11':{file:'grid-system-workspace.js',before:'if(before&&!parameterUseKeys(form).has(before))s.inputPins.delete(before);',after:'if(before&&!parameterSelections(form).some(([,selected])=>selected===before))s.inputPins.delete(before);'},
+  'R353-12':{file:'reference-workspace.js',before:'if(fields.length||card)continue;',after:'if(fields.length)continue;'}
+};
+assert.ok(!mutation||mutations[mutation],'Unknown pin mutation: '+mutation);
+function product(file){
+  let source=fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'geometry-web',file),'utf8');
+  const m=mutations[mutation];
+  if(m?.file===file){assert.equal(source.split(m.before).length,2,'Mutation must match exactly once: '+mutation);source=source.replace(m.before,m.after);}
+  return source;
+}
+const clone=value=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
+const pinKey=r=>'parameter-field:'+r.parameterId+':'+r.fieldId;
+const row=(id,name)=>({id,card:{id,name,fields:[3,8,9].map((field,i)=>({id:field,name:'f'+field,type:'dimension',value:{num:String([600,1200,900][i]),den:'1'}}))}});
+const objects=()=>({ok:true,parameters:[row(10,'A'),row(20,'B')],gridSystems:[],gridBindings:[],faces:[]});
+
+function fixture(currentObjects){
+  const elements=new Map(),prompts=[];
+  let seam;
+  class InputEvent {
+    constructor(type,options={}){this.type=type;this.bubbles=!!options.bubbles;this.stopped=false;}
+    stopImmediatePropagation(){this.stopped=true;}
+    preventDefault(){this.prevented=true;}
+  }
+  const element=id=>{
+    if(elements.has(id))return elements.get(id);
+    const listeners=new Map(),classList={toggle(){},add(){},remove(){}};
+    const node={id,value:'',textContent:'',dataset:{},style:{},classList,options:[],
+      setAttribute(){},removeAttribute(){},replaceChildren(){},click(){},focus(){},append(){},prepend(){},
+      close(){this.open=false;},showModal(){this.open=true;},closest:()=>({classList}),
+      querySelector:()=>({}),querySelectorAll:()=>[],parentElement:{clientWidth:800,clientHeight:600},
+      addEventListener(type,fn){if(!listeners.has(type))listeners.set(type,[]);listeners.get(type).push(fn);},
+      dispatchEvent(event){
+        event.target=this;
+        if(seam?.selectParameterPin(state,event.target)===false){event.stopImmediatePropagation();event.preventDefault();}
+        for(const fn of listeners.get(event.type)||[]){if(event.stopped)break;fn(event);}
+        if(event.bubbles&&!event.stopped)element('dialog')['on'+event.type]?.(event);
+        return !event.prevented;
+      }};
+    elements.set(id,node);return node;
+  };
+  let state;
+  const env={GeometryProject:P,fflate:zip,crypto,structuredClone,JSON,TextEncoder,TextDecoder,Uint8Array,Map,Set,WeakMap,console,AbortSignal,
+    Event:InputEvent,CustomEvent:class {},ResizeObserver:class {observe(){}},File,Blob,
+    requestAnimationFrame:()=>1,pageRuntimeVersion:'pr353-function-probe',checkApiSession(){},
+    window:{dispatchEvent(){},addEventListener(){},confirm:text=>{prompts.push(text);return false;}},
+    document:{body:{append(){}},getElementById:element,querySelectorAll:()=>[],querySelector:()=>element('scroll'),addEventListener(){},createElement:element},
+    oc:{busy:false,faces:[],bmap:{},edit:null,parameters:currentObjects.parameters,gridBindings:[]},fe:{feats:{}},connection:{phase:'ready'},
+    api:async command=>{assert.equal(command,'objects','Only the objects boundary is doubled');return currentObjects;},
+    setStatus(){},toast(){},drawFaceEditor(){},renderFaceInspector(){},innerWidth:1280};
+  env.apiRequest=env.api;
+  vm.runInNewContext(product('reference-workspace.js')+'\nglobalThis.workspace=ReferenceWorkspace;',env);
+  const w=env.workspace;env.ReferenceWorkspace=w;
+  let source=product('grid-system-workspace.js');
+  const start=source.indexOf("    if ($('igCard')) {"),end=source.indexOf('      for (const a of axes) for (const r of dialog.querySelectorAll',start);
+  assert.ok(start>=0&&end>start,'Original card event handler block must be present');
+  const handlers=source.slice(start+"    if ($('igCard')) {".length,end);
+  const bubbleStart=source.indexOf('    dialog.oninput = dialog.onchange = event =>'),bubbleEnd=source.indexOf('    s.normalizedRaw=',bubbleStart);
+  assert.ok(bubbleStart>=0&&bubbleEnd>bubbleStart,'Original input/change persistence handler must be present');
+  // Extract the production handlers without rendering the complete editor.
+  // Only decorative redraws and the persistence handler's presentation wrapper
+  // are doubled; syncCard, cardPins, flush and every lost/pin guard remain real.
+  const install='function installCardHandlers(s){const syncFields=()=>{},drawGrid=()=>{},syncDefinition=()=>{},syncDraftState=s=>{try{flush(s);}catch(error){s.rawError=error.message;}};'+handlers+source.slice(bubbleStart,bubbleEnd)+'}\n';
+  source=source.replace('  window.InfiniteGridWorkspace={',install+'  window.InfiniteGridWorkspace={__pinProbe:{catalogPins,cardPins,selectParameterPin,rawValue,flush,validateDraftPins,installCardHandlers,setState:s=>{state=s;dialog.open=true;}},');
+  vm.runInNewContext(source,env);
+  seam=env.window.InfiniteGridWorkspace.__pinProbe;
+  assert.ok(seam,'Original workspace export seam must be present');
+  return {w,env,element,seam,prompts,
+    start(d,pins){
+      const token=w.getProjectState().contextToken;w.setGridDraft(d.draftId,d,token);
+      for(const [id,value]of Object.entries(d.rawForm))element(id).value=value;
+      state={context:w.context(),contextToken:token,mode:'source',draftId:d.draftId,draftSchema:d.schema,baseRef:null,restored:d,
+        rawOverrides:new Map(),pins,inputPins:new Map(d.inputRefs.map(r=>[pinKey(r),clone(r)])),
+        referenceSelections:new Map(Object.entries(d.rawForm).filter(([id,v])=>v&&id.endsWith('Source')&&!['igUSource','igVSource'].includes(id)).map(([id,v])=>[id,'parameter-field:'+v])),
+        cardPinKeys:d.rawForm.igCard?['igCardU','igCardV'].filter(id=>d.rawForm[id]).map(id=>'parameter-field:'+d.rawForm.igCard+':'+d.rawForm[id]):[],
+        cardSelectionRaw:Object.fromEntries(['igCard','igCardU','igCardV'].map(id=>[id,element(id).value]))};
+      seam.setState(state);seam.installCardHandlers(state);return state;
+    },
+    change(id,value){const node=element(id);node.value=value;node.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input',{bubbles:true}));node.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('change',{bubbles:true}));},
+    swap(){const u=element('igCardU'),v=element('igCardV'),before=u.value;u.value=v.value;v.value=before;u.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('change',{bubbles:true}));}
+  };
+}
+
+async function setup(options={}){
+  const old=objects(),current=clone(old);
+  for(const [id,field]of options.stale||[])current.parameters.find(p=>p.id===id).card.fields.find(f=>f.id===field).value.num+='1';
+  if(options.lost)current.parameters=current.parameters.filter(p=>p.id!==10);
+  const f=fixture(current),catalog=async value=>new Map([...await f.seam.catalogPins(value)].map(([key,ref])=>[key,clone(ref)]));
+  const oldPins=await catalog(old),pins=await catalog(current);
+  const rawForm=Object.assign(Object.fromEntries(P.Features.sourceFields.map(id=>[id,''])),{
+    igName:'pin regression',igMainAlong:'none',igUSource:'repeat',igVSource:'repeat',igUStep:'600',igVStep:'1200',igUPhase:'0',igVPhase:'0'
+  },options.card===false?{}:{igCard:'10',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'8'},options.fields||{});
+  const keys=new Set(Object.entries(rawForm).filter(([id,v])=>v&&id.endsWith('Source')&&!['igUSource','igVSource'].includes(id)).map(([,v])=>'parameter-field:'+v));
+  if(rawForm.igCard)for(const id of ['igCardU','igCardV'])if(rawForm[id])keys.add('parameter-field:'+rawForm.igCard+':'+rawForm[id]);
+  const d={schema:P.Features.GRID_SCHEMA,draftId:'pr353-pins',kind:'source',intent:'create',baseRef:null,
+    rawForm,inputRefs:[...keys].map(key=>({...oldPins.get(key),...(options.lost&&oldPins.get(key).parameterId===10?{lost:true}:{})}))};
+  const s=f.start(d,pins);
+  return {...f,s,d,old,current,oldPins,pins};
+}
+const saved=f=>clone(f.w.readGridDrafts().drafts.find(d=>d.draftId===f.d.draftId));
+const snapshot=f=>JSON.stringify({drafts:f.w.readGridDrafts(),pins:[...f.s.inputPins],raw:f.seam.rawValue(f.s),history:f.w.history.undo.length});
+const getPin=(f,id,field)=>f.s.inputPins.get('parameter-field:'+id+':'+field);
+
+test('R353-10 unchanged V mapping retains stale fingerprint and raw; preview rejects',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({stale:[[10,8]]}),before=saved(f);
+  f.change('igCardU','9');
+  assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,8),before.inputRefs.find(r=>r.fieldId===8));
+  assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igVStep,before.rawForm.igVStep);
+  assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igCardU,'9');assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igCardV,'8');
+  assert.equal(f.prompts.length,0,'Untouched stale V must not be re-acquired');
+  await assert.rejects(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s),/身分已變更|來源.*變更/);
+});
+test('R353-10 input then change cancellation preserves all raw pins and history',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({stale:[[10,8]]}),before=snapshot(f);
+  f.change('igCardU','8');
+  assert.equal(f.prompts.length,1,'Selecting the already-shared stale f8 must ask before overwriting it');
+  assert.equal(snapshot(f),before,'Cancel restores both mappings and leaves input-before-change persistence untouched');
+});
+test('R353-10 explicit stale shared-field selection shows old/new pins before confirmation',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({stale:[[10,8]]}),before=getPin(f,10,8),after=f.pins.get('parameter-field:10:8');
+  f.env.window.confirm=text=>{f.prompts.push(text);assert.ok(text.includes(JSON.stringify(before)));assert.ok(text.includes(JSON.stringify(after)));assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,8),before);return true;};
+  f.change('igCardU','8');
+  assert.equal(f.prompts.length,1);assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,8),after);
+  f.change('igCardU','9');
+  await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s),'After explicit f8 acquisition, a complete distinct mapping validates');
+});
+test('R353-10 swap second confirmation cancellation rolls back both axes atomically',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({stale:[[10,3],[10,8]]}),before=snapshot(f);
+  f.env.window.confirm=text=>{f.prompts.push(text);return f.prompts.length===1;};
+  f.swap();
+  assert.equal(f.prompts.length,2,'Both changed stale sources must be considered before committing either');
+  assert.equal(snapshot(f),before,'Accepting one source then cancelling the other cannot partially repin');
+});
+test('R353-11 phase unlink retains the exact pin still consumed by the whole card',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({fields:{igUPhaseSource:'10:3'}}),before=clone(getPin(f,10,3));
+  f.change('igUPhaseSource','');
+  assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,3),before);assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igUPhaseSource,'');
+  assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igCardU,'3');assert.equal(f.prompts.length,0);
+  await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s));
+});
+test('R353-11 phase unlink preserves a stale whole-card pin without automatic re-acquisition',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({stale:[[10,3]],fields:{igUPhaseSource:'10:3'}}),before=clone(getPin(f,10,3));
+  f.change('igUPhaseSource','');
+  assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,3),before);assert.notDeepEqual(getPin(f,10,3),f.pins.get('parameter-field:10:3'));
+  await assert.rejects(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s),/身分已變更|來源.*變更/);
+});
+test('R353-11 either consumer may leave first; only the last user releases the pin',async()=>{
+  for(const order of ['card-first','field-first']){
+    const f=await setup({fields:{igUPhaseSource:'10:3'}}),before=clone(getPin(f,10,3));
+    const card=()=>f.change('igCardU','9'),field=()=>f.change('igUPhaseSource','');
+    (order==='card-first'?card:field)();assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,3),before,order);
+    (order==='card-first'?field:card)();assert.equal(getPin(f,10,3),undefined,order);
+    assert.ok(getPin(f,10,8));assert.ok(getPin(f,10,9));await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s));
+  }
+});
+test('control: direct and per-field sources retain last-user pin accounting',async()=>{
+  const direct=await setup({card:false});assert.equal(direct.s.inputPins.size,0);await assert.doesNotReject(()=>direct.seam.validateDraftPins(direct.s));
+  const f=await setup({card:false,fields:{igUPhaseSource:'10:3',igVPhaseSource:'10:3'}}),before=clone(getPin(f,10,3));
+  f.change('igUPhaseSource','');assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,10,3),before);await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s));
+  f.change('igVPhaseSource','');assert.equal(f.s.inputPins.size,0);await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s));
+});
+test('R353-12 direct atomic draft replacement accepts B pins already used by both phases',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({lost:true,fields:{igUPhaseSource:'20:3',igVPhaseSource:'20:8'}}),candidate=clone(f.d),history=f.w.history.undo.length;
+  Object.assign(candidate.rawForm,{igCard:'20',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'8'});
+  candidate.inputRefs=candidate.inputRefs.filter(r=>r.parameterId===20);
+  assert.doesNotThrow(()=>f.w.setGridDraft(candidate.draftId,candidate,f.s.contextToken),'A different card is a valid replacement even when both B pins already existed');
+  assert.deepEqual(saved(f),candidate);assert.equal(f.w.history.undo.length,history+1);
+});
+test('R353-12 card selection reuses both existing phase pins with atomic history and ZIP roundtrip',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({lost:true,fields:{igUPhaseSource:'20:3',igVPhaseSource:'20:8'}}),before=saved(f),count=f.w.history.undo.length;
+  const b3=clone(getPin(f,20,3)),b8=clone(getPin(f,20,8));
+  f.change('igCard','20');assert.deepEqual(saved(f),before,'Incomplete map must not replace the persisted lost card');
+  f.change('igCardU','3');assert.deepEqual(saved(f),before,'One axis is not an atomic two-axis replacement');
+  f.change('igCardV','8');const after=saved(f);
+  assert.equal(after.rawForm.igCard,'20');assert.equal(after.rawForm.igCardU,'3');assert.equal(after.rawForm.igCardV,'8');
+  assert.equal(after.rawForm.igUPhaseSource,'20:3');assert.equal(after.rawForm.igVPhaseSource,'20:8');
+  assert.equal(after.inputRefs.length,2);assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,20,3),b3);assert.deepEqual(getPin(f,20,8),b8);
+  assert.equal(f.w.history.undo.length,count+1,'Only the complete mapping writes a single local history step');
+  await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s));
+  assert.equal(f.w.tryLocalHistory('line undo').ok,true);assert.deepEqual(saved(f),before);
+  assert.equal(f.w.tryLocalHistory('line redo').ok,true);assert.deepEqual(saved(f),after);
+  const packed=await P.pack('',await f.w.manifest(),new Map(),zip),cold=await P.unpack(packed,zip);
+  const record=cold.manifest.featureAttachments.records.find(r=>r.kind===P.Features.GRID_KIND);
+  assert.deepEqual(clone(P.Features.gridDrafts(record.value)),clone(f.w.readGridDrafts()));
+});
+test('R353-12 lost replacement rejects incomplete duplicate and unmapped borrowed pins without a write',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({lost:true,fields:{igUPhaseSource:'20:3',igVPhaseSource:'20:8'}}),before=snapshot(f);
+  for(const mapping of [{igCard:'20',igCardU:'3',igCardV:''},{igCard:'20',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'3'},{igCard:'10',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'8'}]){
+    const candidate=clone(f.d);Object.assign(candidate.rawForm,mapping);candidate.inputRefs=candidate.inputRefs.filter(r=>r.parameterId===20);
+    assert.throws(()=>f.w.setGridDraft(candidate.draftId,candidate,f.s.contextToken),/不得純移除|雙欄映射/);
+    assert.equal(snapshot(f),before);
+  }
+});
+test('R353-12 same card and same-ID reconstruction remain rejected after an incomplete selection',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({lost:true}),before=saved(f),history=f.w.history.undo.length;
+  const rebuilt=clone(f.d);rebuilt.inputRefs=rebuilt.inputRefs.map(({lost,...ref})=>ref);
+  assert.throws(()=>f.w.setGridDraft(rebuilt.draftId,rebuilt,f.s.contextToken),/不得抹除/);
+  f.change('igCard','20');f.change('igCard','10');
+  assert.equal(f.element('igCard').value,'20','Owned lost pins must still reject same-ID selection after local pins were removed');
+  assert.deepEqual(saved(f),before);assert.equal(f.w.history.undo.length,history);
+  const sameCard=clone(f.d);sameCard.rawForm.igCardU='9';sameCard.inputRefs=[{...f.oldPins.get('parameter-field:10:9')},sameCard.inputRefs.find(r=>r.fieldId===8)];
+  assert.throws(()=>f.w.setGridDraft(sameCard.draftId,sameCard,f.s.contextToken),/不得純移除|雙欄映射|參數卡/);
+  assert.deepEqual(saved(f),before);
+});
+test('R353-12 replacing a lost pin shared by card and phase must replace both consumer kinds',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({lost:true,fields:{igUPhaseSource:'10:3',igVPhaseSource:'20:8'}}),before=saved(f),candidate=clone(f.d);
+  Object.assign(candidate.rawForm,{igCard:'20',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'8'});
+  candidate.inputRefs=[f.pins.get('parameter-field:20:3'),f.pins.get('parameter-field:20:8')].map(clone);
+  assert.throws(()=>f.w.setGridDraft(candidate.draftId,candidate,f.s.contextToken),/逐欄.*不得純移除/);
+  assert.deepEqual(saved(f),before);
+  candidate.rawForm.igUPhaseSource='20:3';assert.doesNotThrow(()=>f.w.setGridDraft(candidate.draftId,candidate,f.s.contextToken));
+  assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igUPhaseSource,'20:3');assert.equal(saved(f).inputRefs.length,2);
+});
+test('control: original per-field lost replacement may reuse an existing live pin',async()=>{
+  const f=await setup({card:false,lost:true,fields:{igUStepSource:'10:3',igVStepSource:'20:3'}}),before=snapshot(f);
+  f.change('igUStepSource','20:3');assert.equal(snapshot(f),before,'Cancel keeps the original per-field guard');
+  f.env.window.confirm=()=>true;f.change('igUStepSource','20:3');
+  assert.equal(saved(f).inputRefs.length,1);assert.equal(saved(f).rawForm.igUStepSource,'20:3');
+  await assert.doesNotReject(()=>f.seam.validateDraftPins(f.s));
+});
```

P 的前四檔為產品實作；產品架構清單只有一個既有欄位更新：將 `grid_card_entry_test.js` 的 exclusion SHA 同步成 `de2e30ec013f20e5c683f26ffdb989455c9ad562d8b52f14919b81e49e09651e`，避免新測試使既有清單失配。沒有改排除範圍、架構 policy、格式／能力宣告或相容策略。

### 修正行為

| 項目 | 實作與結果 |
|---|---|
| R353-10 | 未明選的軸保留原 pin 與 length raw。明選 stale 共享來源時，以既有 R54 確認顯示 before／after；全部確認成功才提交候選 pins。整卡 select 的 input 不再搶先保存，取消會復原三個映射欄並停止後續保存；swap 第二次取消也不留下第一軸更新。 |
| R353-11 | 統一計算逐欄與整卡 consumer。任一方仍使用時保留同一 pin，最後一個離開才刪；保留 stale 指紋，沒有自動重釘。選取其他欄已持有的 stale pin 同樣先確認。 |
| R353-12 | `setGridDraft` 按舊整卡與新卡 ID／完整雙欄／對應非 lost pins 核對原子替換，接受已由兩軸 phase 使用的 B pins。舊 pin 同時供逐欄及整卡使用時兩種使用關係都要替換。同卡／同 ID 重建仍拒絕，暫選 B 後也會以已保存 draft 的 lost 證據阻擋選回 A。 |
| R353-15 | 正常畫布卡片的摘要、pitch、panel 相位與縮圖共用同一 native row 的 `effectiveStep`／`effectivePanel`；direct／逐欄保留原值路徑。既有 title 顯示整卡 ID 與雙欄映射，沒有改寫來源定義。 |
| R353-16 | repeat 顯示有效值且 card 控制長度為 readonly；panel 保留唯讀呈現。dirty 比較同一有效值，因此未改即可建立配置。只改名仍可預覽／確認，完整保留 `card-reference` 三個 ID 及兩個 `card` token，phase／gap／anchor／stagger 的 param token 也保留。改綁沿用現有完整工作區入口。 |

`公理層.md`、正典閱讀器、`BACKLOG.md`、`決策佇列.md` 均無修改。R353-07／09 仍留下一輪；本補丁沒有更動 reader／格式相容策略，也沒有宣稱相容矩陣已閉合。R353-13／14 的產品碼保持基底內容。

### 已親跑的函式層撤修突變

六種突變均在實際套好 T＋P 的副本上執行，產品原碼只在記憶體中改動；每個 needle 精確命中一次，每次 exit 1，且失敗集中於指定反例。

| 撤修 | 過／失敗 | 對應紅案例 |
|---|---:|---|
| `R353-10` | 13／1 | 未改 V 保留 stale 指紋與 raw |
| `R353-11` | 11／3 | phase 離開仍保留整卡 pin、保留 stale pin、最後使用者才釋放 |
| `R353-12` | 12／2 | 直接原子替換、完整 UI handler 選擇序列都不能再誤拒既有 B pins |
| `canvas_literal` | 21／3 | R353-15 三種軸組合 |
| `inspector_literal` | 21／3 | R353-16 三種軸組合的來源保留／序列化 |
| `inspector_omit_mapping` | 21／3 | R353-16 三種軸組合的 card ID／雙欄映射 |

可在 repo root 個別重跑，以下每行預期 exit 1：

```bash
node tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js . R353-11
node tests/test_pr353_pin_regressions.js . R353-12
node tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js . canvas_literal
node tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js . inspector_literal
node tests/test_pr353_display_regressions.js . inspector_omit_mapping
```

### 本機 browser mutation gate

新 manifest 共 14 組，包含每項至少一個撤修。請在 Linux／WSL 的既有真 native 環境，把 `PR353_BRIDGE`、`PR353_MODEL_BASE` 設為與受測來源相符的實際二進位路徑，`PR353_MUTATION_OUT` 設為 repo 外尚不存在的輸出目錄，再執行：

```bash
python geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py \
  --manifest geometry-web/grid_card_entry_mutations.json \
  --bridge "$PR353_BRIDGE" \
  --model-base "$PR353_MODEL_BASE" \
  --output-dir "$PR353_MUTATION_OUT"
```

runner 沿用 v1 的完整 baseline → mutant → restored、逐案斷言、終態與清理檢查；啟動／fixture／native／transport／cleanup 失敗不能算 killed。此 runner 本輪未親跑。請保留 `receipt.json`、逐次日誌、`P353_CASE_RESULT`／`CDP_RESULT` 與退出碼，據實回報後再推分支。

### 2026-10-28T14:00:44Z ZhiQiYang

【驗證回報】PR #353：R353-10／11／12／15／16 補丁（[1/3](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6469621235)、[2/3](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6469623609)、[3/3](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6469626080)）實跑：函式層全部吻合；瀏覽器 27 案 21 過，6 案卡在兩個測試世界問題，突變未執行

基底 `037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e` ＋ tests-v1 ＋ T ＋ P。**尚未推上分支。**

## 1. 補丁取得

| 補丁 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| T（1/3＋2/3 串接） | 66,634 bytes，SHA-256 `327a6fb6…3eca`，與宣告相同 |
| P（3/3） | 留言內容 24,044 bytes，與宣告的 24,050 不符，`git apply --check` 在 `grid-inspector.js:167` 失敗 |

P 的原因：3/3 留言裡 `grid-inspector.js` 名稱檢查那一行 context 的字元類別，在基底檔案是「反斜線 u 0000、連字號、反斜線 u 001f、反斜線 u 007f」，留言裡前兩個跳脫變成了脫字號記法（`\^@`、`\^_`），全文只有這一處。把這一處換回基底原文後，長度 24,050，SHA-256 `64ae97aa…d2db`，與宣告逐字相同。我用的就是這份還原版，沒有改動任何其他位元組。

**這不是補丁寫錯，看起來是貼到 GitHub 時被轉換**：我這則回報第一次用 `gh` 貼出時，也照抄了那兩個跳脫的原字，讀回後同樣被轉成脫字號記法（已改寫成文字描述）。兩個不同的發文工具結果相同。`\u007f` 沒有被轉。之後補丁若含「反斜線 u」接 0000～001f 的跳脫，請在說明裡標出受影響的檔案與行，我照宣告雜湊還原並回報；或另附 base64。

## 2. 只套 T（對照組）

| 項目 | 結果 |

## 4. 六案未過的原因：都是測試世界，不是產品

**(a) 4 案夾具被原生依 I38 拒絕**：`pin_preserve_untouched`、`pin_reacquire_cancel`、`pin_reacquire_confirm`、`pin_shared_consumers`

- 共同前置 `staleCardDraft()` 先用 `fixture()` 建卡 10（欄位 8、3、13），原生回報 `"nextFieldId":14`。
- 接著 `threeFieldParameter()` 用 `put-parameter` 加入 `{id:9, name:'third dimension'}`，原生回 `valid:false`，訊息原文：`I38 ID: deleted field identities cannot be reused`。
- 9 小於 `nextFieldId` 14，原生把它當成重用已刪除的欄位 id。案例在進入產品流程前就拋 `Fixture candidate rejected`。
- 所以這 4 案**沒有驗到 R353-10／11 的產品行為**。函式層用的是 native 替身，碰不到 I38，這是兩邊結果不同的原因。

**(b) 2 個斷言少了原生回傳的 `kind`**：`REPOINT_SHARED_PHASE_SOURCES`（`lost_card_existing_pins`）、`INSPECTOR_PARAMETER_WITH_DIRECT_EDIT`（`inspector_parameter_control`）

- 測試用 `util.isDeepStrictEqual`，預期值寫 `{parameterId:20,fieldId:3}`。
- 原生 `objects` 實際回傳 `{"kind":"parameter","parameterId":20,"fieldId":3}`。
- 我把 log 裡這兩筆原生值拿出來重算：照測試原寫法是 false；只忽略 `kind` 一個鍵時，兩條斷言的全部條件都成立（u/v phase 指向 20:3、20:8；u.step 引用 10:3、u.step=600、v.step=1300、無 cardReference）。
- 這兩案的其他斷言都通過，失敗只來自這一處。

## 5. 請下一輪交付（只改測試世界，不放寬斷言）

1. 第三個欄位改用未配發過的 id（例如 14，或在第一次建卡時就包含該欄位），連同 4 處 `choose('#igCardU',9)` 與 `assertIdentity(...,10,9,8)` 一起改。
2. 三處預期的 parameterRefs 補上 `kind:'parameter'`，維持深度相等比對。
3. `product-architecture.json` 裡 `grid_card_entry_test.js` 的 exclusion SHA 跟著更新。

我已在暫存複本用上述 1、2 兩處最小改動排隊重跑：27 案＋14 組突變，補丁不推送。結果出來會另貼，可作為你這一輪的對照。

## 基底與套用順序

`feat/r01-grid-card-entry-20261027 @ 037a28267f07503d5b85f6632f57f6e1ea50475e` ＋ tests-v1 ＋ T `327a6fb6…3eca` ＋ P `64ae97aa…d2db` ＋ [已交付 T2](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/353#issuecomment-6489015823) `af776c4bfac7956a3a21a35ed5bb866e5ffce3e1e085ee4ac4855eaea6f4bb04`。發布前重新讀回 PR head 仍為 `037a282`；本包依 T2 後的實際內容重建，不冒稱已有新 head。

本工作拆成三則：**1/3 的 T-node → 2/3 的 T-browser → 3/3 的 P**。每則是獨立可套用 unified diff，不是重新交一份舊 T2。三份已在乾淨的 T2 複本依序 `git apply --check`／`git apply`，12 個輸出檔雜湊全部一致；不推送、不合併。

## 十個具名案例與實際撤修結果

本輪最終 T-node 在真 ZIP codec＋原 `ReferenceWorkspace` staging 的 Node VM 執行：**10/10、fail/skip/cancelled/todo 0，exit 0**。最小 DOM 只提供宿主，不冒充舊版 App E2E。舊 reader 是固定 main `405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676` 的七檔完整 CommonJS 閉包，每次先驗 Git blob；缺歷史或 blob 不符為 blocked，沒有 fallback 到新版。

| 需求／案例前綴 | 具名撤修 | 最終實跑 |
|---|---|---|
| 新 raw 能力：`R35307_RAW_CAPABILITY` | `p353_raw_capability_removed` | 控制 0 → 斷言紅 1 → 還原 0 |
| 舊檔原樣：`R35307_LEGACY_EXACT` | `p353_legacy_raw_normalized` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| 新 raw／rule 重開：`R35307_NEW_RAW_ROUNDTRIP` | `p353_new_raw_mapping_rewritten` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| 剝宣告／偽宣告拒絕：`R35307_DECLARATION_REJECTION` | `p353_missing_raw_declaration_admitted` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| committed／raw-only／mixed：`R35307_MATRIX` | `p353_raw_claims_native_parameter` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| snapshot 內 raw：`R35307_NESTED_RAW` | `p353_nested_raw_capability_removed` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| 真舊 reader 拒絕：`R35307_OLD_READER_ATOMIC_REJECT` | `p353_raw_downgraded_for_old_reader` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| 同一拒絕案的現案保全 | `p353_old_reader_writes_before_rejection` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| 歷史 pins：`R35307_HISTORICAL_PINS` | `p353_historical_pin_recomputed` | 0 → 1 → 0 |
| 實際減項：`R35309_GEO219_SOURCE_REDUCTION` | `p353_geo219_duplicate_paragraph_restored` | 0 → 1 → 0 |

第十個 testcase `R35307_CURRENT_READER_ATOMIC_REJECT` 另核新版對缺宣告檔案的 staging 保全；與缺宣告 mutation、CDP 正式 Open 相互覆蓋。每次 kill 都有完成的同名 assertion failure，無 startup／語法／timeout 冒充。最後整套還原再跑 **10/10**。

固定舊 reader 已支援 committed D64 `/18`，本 T 特別加了 committed-only 仍可讀的正控制；它拒絕的是本次新增的整卡 raw，不能概括成舊 reader 不讀所有 `/18`。

## 只套本輪 T、不套本輪 P

最終 T-only **實跑 10 案：3 通過／7 失敗、exit 1**。七個紅案為：

- `R35307_RAW_CAPABILITY`
- `R35307_NEW_RAW_ROUNDTRIP`
- `R35307_DECLARATION_REJECTION`
- `R35307_MATRIX`
- `R35307_NESTED_RAW`
- `R35307_CURRENT_READER_ATOMIC_REJECT`
- `R35309_GEO219_SOURCE_REDUCTION`

舊檔保留、歷史 pins、舊 reader 既有拒絕本來已部分成立，所以控制組可綠；它們仍各有獨立撤修反證。

## 本機執行

```sh
node --test-reporter=tap tests/test_pr353_compatibility.js
python3 tests/run_pr353_compat_mutations.py --out /tmp/pr353-compat-final
```

輸出目錄必須為新目錄或空目錄。若在無 Git 的 mutation 複本執行，先在有固定 commit 的 repo 跑 `node tests/pr353_legacy_reader.js --materialize <空目錄>`，再把該路徑設為 `GEOMETRY_PR353_LEGACY_ROOT`；七檔仍逐一驗 immutable blob。

## unified diff

```diff
diff --git a/tests/pr353_legacy_reader.js b/tests/pr353_legacy_reader.js
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/pr353_legacy_reader.js
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+'use strict';
+/* Fixed real pre-PR353 archive reader. Never fall back to today's implementation.
+ * The optional root supplies original files when this checkout is shallow/offline.
+ * Every source is checked against the immutable Git blob before execution.
+ */
+const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),vm=require('node:vm');
+const {createHash}=require('node:crypto'),{execFileSync}=require('node:child_process');
+const Module=require('node:module');
+const LEGACY_COMMIT='405f8c5e1a637a16cbe47470faf5ae1fa02b8676';
+const LEGACY_BLOBS=Object.freeze({
+  "geometry-web/project-store.js": "961088f6fe27433f95f9e425d333a90352cd59dd",
+  "geometry-web/feature-attachments.js": "7db220ab5cd528aaacef41747b8360452145131a",
+  "geometry-web/scenario-catalog.js": "b0ef33f08ddc20bb33c5a0b278a1a395037a5724",
+  "geometry-web/rule-case-data.js": "70851c203947c054ad10861a65f5ba07715da67c",
+  "geometry-web/source-plane.js": "5f985604472f4dda5c0000318326b08dc33e44cd",
+  "geometry-web/evidence-links.js": "c2ce6a6c6fdcab2ffbdd7aa495282f72fb28b728",
+  "geometry-web/reference-display.js": "8210d4428e44a9b8edf0dd4c4073ba6667076f0e"
+});
+function readLegacySources() {
+  const checkout=path.resolve(__dirname,'..'),provided=process.env.GEOMETRY_PR353_LEGACY_ROOT;
+  const sources={};
+  for(const [file,expected]of Object.entries(LEGACY_BLOBS)){
+    let bytes;
+    try { bytes=provided?fs.readFileSync(path.join(provided,file)):execFileSync('git',['show',LEGACY_COMMIT+':'+file],{cwd:checkout,maxBuffer:2*1024*1024,stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe']}); }
+    catch(error){throw Error('PR353 legacy reader precondition: exact '+LEGACY_COMMIT+' source unavailable: '+file+'. Supply GEOMETRY_PR353_LEGACY_ROOT or fetch the fixed commit. '+error.message);}
+    const actual=createHash('sha1').update(Buffer.from('blob '+bytes.length+String.fromCharCode(0))).update(bytes).digest('hex');
+    if(actual!==expected)throw Error('PR353 legacy reader precondition: Git blob mismatch for '+file+': '+actual+' != '+expected);
+    sources[file]=bytes.toString('utf8');
+  }
+  return sources;
+}
+function loadLegacyReader(){
+  const sources=readLegacySources(),cache=new Map();
+  function load(file){
+    if(cache.has(file))return cache.get(file).exports;
+    if(!Object.hasOwn(sources,file))throw Error('PR353 legacy reader dependency outside fixed closure: '+file);
+    const module={exports:{}};
+    cache.set(file,module);
+    const filename=path.resolve(__dirname,'..',file);
+    const requireFixed=request=>{
+      if(!request.startsWith('./'))throw Error('PR353 legacy reader unexpected dependency: '+request);
+      return load(path.posix.normalize(path.posix.join(path.posix.dirname(file),request)));
+    };
+    vm.runInThisContext(Module.wrap(sources[file]),{filename:LEGACY_COMMIT+'/'+file})(module.exports,requireFixed,module,filename,path.dirname(filename));
+    return module.exports;
+  }
+  return load('geometry-web/project-store.js');
+}
+function materializeLegacyReader(destination){
+  const sources=readLegacySources(),target=path.resolve(destination),receipt=[];
+  if(fs.existsSync(target)&&fs.readdirSync(target).length)throw Error('Legacy reader destination must be empty.');
+  for(const [file,source]of Object.entries(sources)){
+    const filename=path.join(target,file),bytes=Buffer.from(source,'utf8');
+    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filename),{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(filename,bytes,{flag:'wx'});
+    receipt.push({file,gitBlob:LEGACY_BLOBS[file],bytes:bytes.length,sha256:createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')});
+  }
+  return {commit:LEGACY_COMMIT,root:target,files:receipt};
+}
+module.exports={LEGACY_COMMIT,LEGACY_BLOBS,readLegacySources,loadLegacyReader,loadLegacyProject:loadLegacyReader,materializeLegacyReader};
+if(require.main===module){
+  if(process.argv.length!==4||process.argv[2]!=='--materialize')throw Error('Usage: node tests/pr353_legacy_reader.js --materialize EMPTY_DIRECTORY');
+  console.log(JSON.stringify(materializeLegacyReader(process.argv[3]),null,2));
+}
diff --git a/tests/test_pr353_compatibility.js b/tests/test_pr353_compatibility.js
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_pr353_compatibility.js
@@ -0,0 +1,187 @@
+'use strict';
+/* Real ZIP/current + fixed 405f8c5 reader contracts. The staging fixture uses
+ * production ReferenceWorkspace with a minimal DOM; it is not browser/native E2E.
+ */
+const {test}=require('node:test'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
+const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),vm=require('node:vm');
+const P=require('../geometry-web/project-store.js'),F=P.Features;
+const zip=require('../geometry-web/vendor/fflate/umd/index.js');
+const L=require('./pr353_legacy_reader.js');
+const old=L.loadLegacyReader(),CAP='grid-card-draft/1';
+const copy=structuredClone;
+const ordinary='grid-system 4 name-hex 47 main-along none u repeat -5 600 v repeat 7 400\n';
+const perField='requires parameters/1\nparameters begin\nparameters end\ngrid-system 4 name-hex 47 main-along none u repeat -5 param:10:3 v repeat 7 param:10:8\n';
+const committed='requires parameters/1\nrequires grid-card-reference/1\nparameters begin\nparameters end\ngrid-system 4 name-hex 47 main-along none card-reference 10 3 8 u repeat -5 card v repeat 7 card\n';
+const nativeRule=committed+'requires grid-subdivision-rules/1\ngrid-source-rule 4 key 7 name-hex 72756c65 selector cell -2 1 divide 3 2\ngrid-source-rule-highwater 4 next 11\n';
+function draft({card=true,rules=false,id='raw-card',lost=false,edit=false}={}){
+  const rawForm=Object.fromEntries(F.sourceFields.map(key=>[key,'']));
+  Object.assign(rawForm,{igName:'  未確認 <&>  ',igMainAlong:'none',igUSource:'repeat',igVSource:'repeat',igUStep:' 0600 ',igVStep:' 0400 ',igUPhase:' - ',igVPhase:' 007 '});
+  if(card)Object.assign(rawForm,{igCard:'10',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'8'});
+  const inputRefs=[{kind:'parameter-field',parameterId:10,fieldId:3,fingerprint:'a'.repeat(64)},
+    {kind:'parameter-field',parameterId:10,fieldId:8,fingerprint:'b'.repeat(64)}];
+  if(lost)inputRefs[1].lost=true;
+  return {schema:rules?F.GRID_RULE_SCHEMA:F.GRID_SCHEMA,draftId:id,kind:'source',intent:rules||edit?'edit':'create',
+    baseRef:rules||edit?{kind:'grid-system',id:4,fingerprint:'c'.repeat(64)}:null,inputRefs,rawForm,
+    ...(rules?{ruleDraft:{intent:'create',ruleKey:null,rawForm:{name:' source rule ',selector:'cell',cellI:' 9007199254740993 ',cellJ:' - ',divisionsU:' 3 ',divisionsV:''}}}:{})};
+}
+function envelope(value){return {schema:F.SCHEMA,records:[{kind:F.GRID_KIND,schema:value.schema,value}]};}
+function manifest(value){return {format:P.FORMAT,projectId:'r353-compat-project',name:'compatibility',sceneHash:'0'.repeat(64),sources:[],faces:[],...(value?{featureAttachments:envelope(value)}:{})};}
+const pack=(scene,value,reader=P)=>reader.pack(scene,manifest(value),new Map(),zip);
+const reopen=async(scene,value,reader=P)=>P.unpack(await pack(scene,value,reader),zip);
+const caps=value=>value.manifest.requiredCapabilities||[];
+function rewrite(bytes,fn){const files=zip.unzipSync(bytes),m=JSON.parse(zip.strFromU8(files['manifest.json']));fn(m);files['manifest.json']=zip.strToU8(JSON.stringify(m));return zip.zipSync(files);}
+const draftValues=opened=>F.gridDrafts(opened.manifest.featureAttachments.records.find(record=>record.kind===F.GRID_KIND).value);
+
+test('R35307_RAW_CAPABILITY presence includes empty and unknown mapping without native claims',async()=>{
+  assert.equal(F.GRID_CARD_CAPABILITY,CAP);
+  for(const mapping of [{igCard:'10',igCardU:'3',igCardV:'8'},{igCard:'',igCardU:'',igCardV:''},{igCard:'missing',igCardU:' 999 ',igCardV:'?'}]){
+    const raw=draft();Object.assign(raw.rawForm,mapping);
+    const before=copy(raw),opened=await reopen('reset\n',raw);
+    assert.ok(caps(opened).includes(CAP),'raw-card capability must be derived from fields, not truthiness');
+    assert.equal(caps(opened).includes(P.CARD_CAPABILITY),false,'raw is not a committed native card');
+    assert.equal(opened.manifest.format,P.FEATURE_FORMAT);
+    assert.deepEqual(opened.manifest.featureAttachments,envelope(before));
+    P.assertProjectCapabilities(opened.manifest,P.readerCapabilities([]));
+    assert.throws(()=>P.assertProjectCapabilities(opened.manifest,P.readerCapabilities([]).filter(cap=>cap!==CAP)));
+    assert.equal(P.nativeReaderCapabilities([CAP]).includes(CAP),false,'editor capability is not a native claim');
+    assert.throws(()=>P.validateProjectCapabilities([CAP]));
+  }
+});
+test('R35307_LEGACY_EXACT fixed old writer /1 /2 collection and direct/ParameterRef remain unchanged',async()=>{
+  for(const scene of [ordinary,perField])for(const rules of [false,true]){
+    const raw=draft({card:false,rules}),before=copy(raw),bytes=await pack(scene,raw,old);
+    const oldOpened=await old.unpack(bytes,zip),opened=await P.unpack(bytes,zip);
+    assert.deepEqual(opened.manifest,oldOpened.manifest,'current reader cannot migrate old manifest');
+    assert.equal(opened.scene,scene);assert.deepEqual(draftValues(opened).drafts,[before]);
+    assert.equal(caps(opened).includes(CAP),false);
+    assert.equal(caps(opened).includes(P.CARD_CAPABILITY),false,'same-card fields do not synthesize card semantics');
+    const again=await P.unpack(await P.pack(opened.scene,opened.manifest,opened.sources,zip),zip);
+    assert.deepEqual(again.manifest,oldOpened.manifest,'resave preserves historical draft schema and pins');
+  }
+  const collection={schema:F.GRID_DRAFTS_SCHEMA,drafts:[draft({card:false,id:'one'}),draft({card:false,rules:true,id:'two'})],orphans:[]};
+  const bytes=await pack(ordinary,collection,old),opened=await P.unpack(bytes,zip);
+  assert.equal(opened.manifest.format,P.GRID_DRAFTS_FORMAT);assert.deepEqual(draftValues(opened),{drafts:collection.drafts,orphans:[]});
+  assert.equal(caps(opened).includes(CAP),false);
+});
+test('R35307_NEW_RAW_ROUNDTRIP card plus source-rule raw strings and missing pins remain exact',async()=>{
+  for(const rules of [false,true]){
+    const raw=draft({rules});raw.inputRefs=[];raw.rawForm.igCardV=' unknown ';
+    const before=copy(raw),opened=await reopen(rules?nativeRule:perField,raw);
+    assert.equal(opened.scene,rules?nativeRule:perField);
+    assert.deepEqual(draftValues(opened).drafts,[before]);
+    assert.ok(caps(opened).includes(CAP));
+    const second=await P.unpack(await P.pack(opened.scene,opened.manifest,opened.sources,zip),zip);
+    assert.deepEqual(second.manifest,opened.manifest);assert.deepEqual(raw,before,'writer does not repair caller raw');
+  }
+});
+test('R35307_DECLARATION_REJECTION stripped false and unknown raw capabilities fail before admission',async()=>{
+  const bytes=await pack('reset\n',draft());
+  await assert.rejects(()=>P.unpack(rewrite(bytes,m=>m.requiredCapabilities=m.requiredCapabilities.filter(cap=>cap!==CAP)),zip),/能力/);
+  await assert.rejects(()=>P.unpack(rewrite(bytes,m=>m.requiredCapabilities=m.requiredCapabilities.map(cap=>cap===CAP?'grid-card-draft/999':cap)),zip),/能力/);
+  const noRaw=await pack(ordinary,null);
+  await assert.rejects(()=>P.unpack(rewrite(noRaw,m=>{m.format=P.FEATURE_FORMAT;m.models=[];m.faceProjections=[];m.requiredCapabilities=[CAP];}),zip));
+  const legacy=await pack(ordinary,draft({card:false}));
+  await assert.rejects(()=>P.unpack(rewrite(legacy,m=>m.requiredCapabilities.push(CAP)),zip),/能力/);
+  for(const field of ['igCard','igCardU','igCardV']){
+    await assert.rejects(()=>P.unpack(rewrite(bytes,m=>delete m.featureAttachments.records[0].value.rawForm[field]),zip),/欄位/);
+  }
+});
+test('R35307_MATRIX committed raw-only mixed and mixed collections require only their actual semantics',async()=>{
+  const cases=[
+    {name:'committed',scene:committed,raw:null,format:P.CARD_FORMAT,required:[P.CARD_CAPABILITY,P.PARAMETER_CAPABILITY]},
+    {name:'raw-only',scene:'reset\n',raw:draft(),format:P.FEATURE_FORMAT,required:[CAP,F.GRID_SCHEMA]},
+    {name:'raw-rule-only',scene:ordinary,raw:draft({rules:true}),format:P.GRID_RULE_FORMAT,required:[CAP,F.GRID_RULE_SCHEMA]},
+    {name:'mixed',scene:committed,raw:draft(),format:P.CARD_FORMAT,required:[CAP,P.CARD_CAPABILITY,F.GRID_SCHEMA,P.PARAMETER_CAPABILITY]},
+    {name:'mixed-rules',scene:nativeRule,raw:draft({rules:true}),format:P.CARD_FORMAT,required:[CAP,P.CARD_CAPABILITY,F.GRID_RULE_SCHEMA,P.SUBDIVISION_CAPABILITY,P.PARAMETER_CAPABILITY]},
+    {name:'raw-collection',scene:'reset\n',raw:{schema:F.GRID_DRAFTS_SCHEMA,drafts:[draft({card:false,id:'a-old'}),draft({id:'b-new',rules:true})],orphans:[]},format:P.GRID_DRAFTS_FORMAT,required:[CAP,F.GRID_SCHEMA,F.GRID_RULE_SCHEMA,F.GRID_DRAFTS_SCHEMA]}
+  ];
+  for(const c of cases){
+    const opened=await reopen(c.scene,c.raw);
+    assert.equal(opened.manifest.format,c.format,c.name);
+    assert.deepEqual(caps(opened),c.required.sort(),c.name);
+    assert.equal(opened.scene,c.scene,c.name);
+    if(c.raw)assert.deepEqual(opened.manifest.featureAttachments,envelope(c.raw),c.name);
+  }
+});
+test('R35307_NESTED_RAW scenario-only raw propagates capability to root and cannot strip it',async()=>{
+  const S=require('../geometry-web/scenario-snapshots.js'),raw=draft({rules:true}),leaf=await reopen(ordinary,raw);
+  const snapshots=S.create({project:P,featureAttachments:{validate:F.validate,references:F.sourceIds},
+    verifyProject:async()=>({ok:true,capabilities:[],final_objects:{boundaries:[],placements:[]},objects:[]})});
+  await snapshots.capture(leaf,{scenarioId:'raw-snapshot',label:'raw-snapshot'});
+  const root={...manifest(null),scenarioCatalog:{schema:S.SCHEMA,scenarios:snapshots.list()}};
+  const bytes=await P.pack('reset\n',root,new Map(),zip),opened=await P.unpack(bytes,zip);
+  assert.ok(caps(opened).includes(CAP),'root must advertise nested raw');
+  assert.equal(caps(opened).includes(P.CARD_CAPABILITY),false);
+  assert.deepEqual(opened.manifest.scenarioCatalog.scenarios[0].snapshot.projectManifest,leaf.manifest);
+  await assert.rejects(()=>P.unpack(rewrite(bytes,m=>m.requiredCapabilities=m.requiredCapabilities.filter(cap=>cap!==CAP)),zip),/能力/);
+});
+function workspace(reader){
+  const elements=new Map(),calls=[],statuses=[];
+  const element=id=>{
+    if(!elements.has(id))elements.set(id,{textContent:'',innerHTML:'',dataset:{},style:{},value:'',hidden:false,
+      classList:{toggle(){}},setAttribute(){},removeAttribute(){},replaceChildren(){},append(){},prepend(){},
+      addEventListener(){},querySelector:()=>null,querySelectorAll:()=>[],parentElement:{clientWidth:800,clientHeight:600}});
+    return elements.get(id);
+  };
+  const env={GeometryProject:reader,fflate:zip,crypto,structuredClone,JSON,TextEncoder,TextDecoder,Uint8Array,Map,Set,WeakMap,console,AbortSignal,
+    requestAnimationFrame:()=>1,CustomEvent:class{},ResizeObserver:class{observe(){}},File,Blob,
+    pageRuntimeVersion:'r353-compat-vm',checkApiSession(){},
+    fetch:async(url)=>{calls.push(url);throw Error('Unexpected upload/fetch after archive refusal: '+url);},
+    window:{dispatchEvent(){},addEventListener(){},confirm:()=>false},
+    document:{getElementById:element,querySelectorAll:()=>[],querySelector:()=>null,addEventListener(){},createElement:tag=>element(tag)},
+    oc:{busy:false,faces:[],bmap:{},edit:null},fe:{feats:{}},connection:{phase:'ready',session:'r353-fixed-session'},
+    api:async command=>{calls.push(command);throw Error('Unexpected native command after archive refusal: '+command);},
+    setStatus:s=>statuses.push(s),toast:s=>statuses.push(s),drawFaceEditor(){},renderFaceInspector(){},innerWidth:1280};
+  const file=path.resolve(__dirname,'../geometry-web/reference-workspace.js');
+  vm.runInNewContext(fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8')+'\nglobalThis.workspace=ReferenceWorkspace;',env,{filename:file});
+  const w=env.workspace,c=w.context();
+  c.name='Existing project must survive';c.dirty=true;c.provenance.kind='new';
+  c.featureAttachments=[old.Features.record(old.Features.GRID_KIND,draft({card:false,id:'existing'}))];
+  w.history.push(w.history.snapshot(),'local','Existing undo entry');
+  const initial={context:w.context(),frame:w.history.frame,state:copy(w.getProjectState()),
+    snapshot:JSON.stringify(w.history.snapshot()),undo:w.history.undo.length,redo:w.history.redo.length,raw:JSON.stringify(w.readGridDrafts())};
+  return {w,calls,statuses,initial};
+}
+test('R35307_OLD_READER_ATOMIC_REJECT actual fixed reader refuses raw and mixed before native context/history writes',async()=>{
+  const committedOnly=await pack(committed,null),oldCommitted=await old.unpack(committedOnly,zip);
+  assert.equal(oldCommitted.scene,committed,'fixed pre-PR353 reader still supports committed D64');
+  assert.equal((oldCommitted.manifest.requiredCapabilities||[]).includes(CAP),false);
+  for(const scene of ['reset\n',committed]){
+    const bytes=await pack(scene,draft());
+    await assert.rejects(()=>old.unpack(bytes,zip),/版本|能力|欄位/);
+    const {w,calls,initial}=workspace(old);
+    await assert.rejects(()=>w.importBytes(bytes,'new-card.gcanvas'),/版本|能力|欄位/);
+    assert.equal(w.context(),initial.context);assert.equal(w.history.frame,initial.frame);
+    assert.deepEqual(copy(w.getProjectState()),initial.state);
+    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(w.history.snapshot()),initial.snapshot);
+    assert.equal(w.history.undo.length,initial.undo);assert.equal(w.history.redo.length,initial.redo);
+    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(w.readGridDrafts()),initial.raw);
+    assert.deepEqual(calls,[],'no native or upload request precedes archive admission');

+test('R35307_CURRENT_READER_ATOMIC_REJECT missing editor capability keeps existing project and history',async()=>{
+  const bytes=await pack('reset\n',draft()),stripped=rewrite(bytes,m=>m.requiredCapabilities=m.requiredCapabilities.filter(cap=>cap!==CAP));
+  const {w,calls,initial}=workspace(P);

+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(w.history.snapshot()),initial.snapshot);assert.deepEqual(copy(w.getProjectState()),initial.state);
+  assert.deepEqual(calls,[]);
+});
+test('R35307_HISTORICAL_PINS all fingerprints lost flags and orphan status survive repeated archive reads',async()=>{
+  const first=draft({lost:true,edit:true}),second=draft({card:false,rules:true,id:'legacy-rule'});
+  second.baseRef.id=5;
+  const value={schema:F.GRID_DRAFTS_SCHEMA,drafts:[first,second],orphans:[first.draftId]},before=copy(value);

+  assert.equal(F.gridSourceKey(source,true),old.Features.gridSourceKey(source,true));
+  assert.equal(F.gridSourceKey(source),old.Features.gridSourceKey(source));
+});
+test('R35309_GEO219_SOURCE_REDUCTION one instruction paragraph replaces two without removing information',()=>{
```
